// CUSTOMER MASTER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all customers
function handleGetCustomersGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all customers from CustomerMaster sheet
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var customers = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < customerData.length; i++) {
    if (customerData[i][0]) { // Check if Code exists
      customers.push({
        code: customerData[i][0],
        description: customerData[i][1] || '',
        trn: customerData[i][2] || '',
        tel1: customerData[i][3] || '',
        tel2: customerData[i][4] || '',
        mobile: customerData[i][5] || '',
        email1: customerData[i][6] || '',
        email2: customerData[i][7] || '',
        homePage: customerData[i][8] || '',
        addressStreet: customerData[i][9] || '',
        addressCity: customerData[i][10] || '',
        addressEmirate: customerData[i][11] || '',
        addressPO: customerData[i][12] || '',
        addressCountry: customerData[i][13] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", customers: customers };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get the next auto-generated customer code
function handleGetNextCustomerCodeGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read the full CustomerMaster sheet to find the maximum CUST numeric code
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number (matches existing CUST001+ format)
  
  for (var i = 1; i < customerData.length; i++) {
    if (customerData[i][0]) {
      var code = customerData[i][0].toString();
      if (code.toUpperCase().startsWith('CUST')) {
        var num = parseInt(code.substring(4));
        if (!isNaN(num) && num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Next number is maxNum + 1
  var nextCode = 'CUST' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  var result = { status: "success", nextCode: nextCode };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new customer
function handleCreateCustomerGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var trn = e.parameter.trn || '';
  var tel1 = e.parameter.tel1 || '';
  var tel2 = e.parameter.tel2 || '';
  var mobile = e.parameter.mobile || '';
  var email1 = e.parameter.email1 || '';
  var email2 = e.parameter.email2 || '';
  var homePage = e.parameter.homePage || '';
  var addressStreet = e.parameter.addressStreet || '';
  var addressCity = e.parameter.addressCity || '';
  var addressEmirate = e.parameter.addressEmirate || '';
  var addressPO = e.parameter.addressPO || '';
  var addressCountry = e.parameter.addressCountry || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Description is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // If code is not provided, auto-generate it from the full CustomerMaster sheet
  if (!code) {
    var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number
    for (var i = 1; i < customerData.length; i++) {
      if (customerData[i][0]) {
        var existingCode = customerData[i][0].toString();
        if (existingCode.toUpperCase().startsWith('CUST')) {
          var num = parseInt(existingCode.substring(4));
          if (!isNaN(num) && num > maxNum) {
            maxNum = num;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    code = 'CUST' + (maxNum + 1);
  }
  
  // Validate code format (CUST + numbers)
  var codePattern = /^CUST\d+$/i;
  if (!codePattern.test(code)) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Customer code must follow format: CUST + numbers (e.g., CUST001)" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if customer code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < customerData.length; i++) {
    if (customerData[i][0] && customerData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Customer code already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new customer
  customerSheet.appendRow([
    code.toUpperCase(),
    description,
    trn,
    tel1,
    tel2,
    mobile,
    email1,
    email2,
    homePage,
    addressStreet,
    addressCity,
    addressEmirate,
    addressPO,
    addressCountry
  ]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Customer created successfully", code: code.toUpperCase() };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing customer
function handleUpdateCustomerGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var trn = e.parameter.trn || '';
  var tel1 = e.parameter.tel1 || '';
  var tel2 = e.parameter.tel2 || '';
  var mobile = e.parameter.mobile || '';
  var email1 = e.parameter.email1 || '';
  var email2 = e.parameter.email2 || '';
  var homePage = e.parameter.homePage || '';
  var addressStreet = e.parameter.addressStreet || '';
  var addressCity = e.parameter.addressCity || '';
  var addressEmirate = e.parameter.addressEmirate || '';
  var addressPO = e.parameter.addressPO || '';
  var addressCountry = e.parameter.addressCountry || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Code and Description are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and update customer
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < customerData.length; i++) {
    if (customerData[i][0] && customerData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      customerSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 14).setValues([[
        code.toUpperCase(),
        description,
        trn,
        tel1,
        tel2,
        mobile,
        email1,
        email2,
        homePage,
        addressStreet,
        addressCity,
        addressEmirate,
        addressPO,
        addressCountry
      ]]);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Customer not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Customer updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete customer
function handleDeleteCustomerGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find the customer first
  var found = false;
  var customerRowIndex = -1;
  for (var i = 1; i < customerData.length; i++) {
    if (customerData[i][0] && customerData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      customerRowIndex = i + 1;
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Customer not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check for transaction/history references before deleting.
  // If references exist, block the delete to preserve financial/audit history.
  var refs = countCustomerReferences(ss, code);
  var refParts = [];
  if (refs.salesHeaders > 0) refParts.push(refs.salesHeaders + (refs.salesHeaders === 1 ? " sale" : " sales"));
  if (refs.accountTransactions > 0) refParts.push(refs.accountTransactions + (refs.accountTransactions === 1 ? " accounting entry" : " accounting entries"));
  if (refs.journalVoucherDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.journalVoucherDetails + (refs.journalVoucherDetails === 1 ? " journal voucher line" : " journal voucher lines"));
  
  if (refParts.length > 0) {
    var msg = "Cannot delete: Customer '" + code + "' is referenced in " + refParts.join(", ") + ". Delete or unlink these records first.";
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: msg };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // No references found - delete the customer
  customerSheet.deleteRow(customerRowIndex);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Customer deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Count references to a customer code across transaction/history sheets
function countCustomerReferences(ss, code) {
  var refs = {
    salesHeaders: 0,
    accountTransactions: 0,
    journalVoucherDetails: 0
  };
  
  if (!code) return refs;
  
  var codeUpper = code.toString().toUpperCase();
  
  // SalesHeader sheet - customer at index 3
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  if (salesHeaderSheet) {
    var salesData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < salesData.length; i++) {
      if (salesData[i][3] && salesData[i][3].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.salesHeaders++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // AccountTransaction sheet - subledger (SUBLEDGER_DOCNO) at index 4
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var txData = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < txData.length; i++) {
      if (txData[i][4] && txData[i][4].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.accountTransactions++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // JournalVoucherDetails sheet - Subledger at index 5
  var jvDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (jvDetailsSheet) {
    var jvData = jvDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < jvData.length; i++) {
      if (jvData[i][5] && jvData[i][5].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.journalVoucherDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return refs;
}

// ============================================
// SUPPLIER MASTER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all suppliers
function handleGetSuppliersGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all suppliers from SupplierMaster sheet
  var supplierSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SupplierMaster");
  var supplierData = supplierSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var suppliers = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < supplierData.length; i++) {
    if (supplierData[i][0]) { // Check if Code exists
      suppliers.push({
        code: supplierData[i][0],
        description: supplierData[i][1] || '',
        trn: supplierData[i][2] || '',
        tel1: supplierData[i][3] || '',
        tel2: supplierData[i][4] || '',
        mobile: supplierData[i][5] || '',
        email1: supplierData[i][6] || '',
        email2: supplierData[i][7] || '',
        homePage: supplierData[i][8] || '',
        addressStreet: supplierData[i][9] || '',
        addressCity: supplierData[i][10] || '',
        addressEmirate: supplierData[i][11] || '',
        addressPO: supplierData[i][12] || '',
        addressCountry: supplierData[i][13] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", suppliers: suppliers };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get the next auto-generated supplier code
function handleGetNextSupplierCodeGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read the full SupplierMaster sheet to find the maximum SUPP numeric code
  var supplierSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SupplierMaster");
  var supplierData = supplierSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number (matches existing SUPP001+ format)
  
  for (var i = 1; i < supplierData.length; i++) {
    if (supplierData[i][0]) {
      var code = supplierData[i][0].toString();
      if (code.toUpperCase().startsWith('SUPP')) {
        var num = parseInt(code.substring(4));
        if (!isNaN(num) && num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Next number is maxNum + 1
  var nextCode = 'SUPP' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  var result = { status: "success", nextCode: nextCode };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new supplier
function handleCreateSupplierGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var trn = e.parameter.trn || '';
  var tel1 = e.parameter.tel1 || '';
  var tel2 = e.parameter.tel2 || '';
  var mobile = e.parameter.mobile || '';
  var email1 = e.parameter.email1 || '';
  var email2 = e.parameter.email2 || '';
  var homePage = e.parameter.homePage || '';
  var addressStreet = e.parameter.addressStreet || '';
  var addressCity = e.parameter.addressCity || '';
  var addressEmirate = e.parameter.addressEmirate || '';
  var addressPO = e.parameter.addressPO || '';
  var addressCountry = e.parameter.addressCountry || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Description is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var supplierSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SupplierMaster");
  var supplierData = supplierSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // If code is not provided, auto-generate it from the full SupplierMaster sheet
  if (!code) {
    var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number
    for (var i = 1; i < supplierData.length; i++) {
      if (supplierData[i][0]) {
        var existingCode = supplierData[i][0].toString();
        if (existingCode.toUpperCase().startsWith('SUPP')) {
          var num = parseInt(existingCode.substring(4));
          if (!isNaN(num) && num > maxNum) {
            maxNum = num;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    code = 'SUPP' + (maxNum + 1);
  }
  
  // Validate code format (SUPP + numbers)
  var codePattern = /^SUPP\d+$/i;
  if (!codePattern.test(code)) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Supplier code must follow format: SUPP + numbers (e.g., SUPP001)" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if supplier code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < supplierData.length; i++) {
    if (supplierData[i][0] && supplierData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Supplier code already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new supplier
  supplierSheet.appendRow([
    code.toUpperCase(),
    description,
    trn,
    tel1,
    tel2,
    mobile,
    email1,
    email2,
    homePage,
    addressStreet,
    addressCity,
    addressEmirate,
    addressPO,
    addressCountry
  ]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Supplier created successfully", code: code.toUpperCase() };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing supplier
function handleUpdateSupplierGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;

  var description = e.parameter.description;
