// Delete supplier
function handleDeleteSupplierGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var supplierSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SupplierMaster");
  var supplierData = supplierSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find the supplier first
  var found = false;
  var supplierRowIndex = -1;
  for (var i = 1; i < supplierData.length; i++) {
    if (supplierData[i][0] && supplierData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      supplierRowIndex = i + 1;
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Supplier not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check for transaction/history references before deleting.
  // If references exist, block the delete to preserve financial/audit history.
  var refs = countSupplierReferences(ss, code);
  var refParts = [];
  if (refs.purchaseHeaders > 0) refParts.push(refs.purchaseHeaders + (refs.purchaseHeaders === 1 ? " purchase" : " purchases"));
  if (refs.accountTransactions > 0) refParts.push(refs.accountTransactions + (refs.accountTransactions === 1 ? " accounting entry" : " accounting entries"));
  if (refs.journalVoucherDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.journalVoucherDetails + (refs.journalVoucherDetails === 1 ? " journal voucher line" : " journal voucher lines"));
  
  if (refParts.length > 0) {
    var msg = "Cannot delete: Supplier '" + code + "' is referenced in " + refParts.join(", ") + ". Delete or unlink these records first.";
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: msg };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // No references found - delete the supplier
  supplierSheet.deleteRow(supplierRowIndex);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Supplier deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Count references to a supplier code across transaction/history sheets
function countSupplierReferences(ss, code) {
  var refs = {
    purchaseHeaders: 0,
    accountTransactions: 0,
    journalVoucherDetails: 0
  };
  
  if (!code) return refs;
  
  var codeUpper = code.toString().toUpperCase();
  
  // PurchaseHeader sheet - supplier at index 3
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  if (purchaseHeaderSheet) {
    var purchaseData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < purchaseData.length; i++) {
      if (purchaseData[i][3] && purchaseData[i][3].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.purchaseHeaders++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // AccountTransaction sheet - subledger (SUBLEDGER_DOCNO) at index 4
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var txData = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < txData.length; i++) {
      if (txData[i][4] && txData[i][4].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.accountTransactions++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // JournalVoucherDetails sheet - Subledger at index 5
  var jvDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (jvDetailsSheet) {
    var jvData = jvDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < jvData.length; i++) {
      if (jvData[i][5] && jvData[i][5].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.journalVoucherDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return refs;
}

// ============================================
// SALESMAN MASTER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all salesmen
function handleGetSalesmenGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all salesmen from Salesman sheet
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var salesmen = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesmanData.length; i++) {
    if (salesmanData[i][0]) { // Check if Code exists
      salesmen.push({
        code: salesmanData[i][0],
        description: salesmanData[i][1] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", salesmen: salesmen };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get the next auto-generated salesman code
function handleGetNextSalesmanCodeGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read the full Salesman sheet to find the maximum SM numeric code
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number (high enough to avoid collisions with existing SM001-format codes)
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesmanData.length; i++) {
    if (salesmanData[i][0]) {
      var code = salesmanData[i][0].toString();
      if (code.toUpperCase().startsWith('SM')) {
        var num = parseInt(code.substring(2));
        if (!isNaN(num) && num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Next number is maxNum + 1
  var nextCode = 'SM' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  var result = { status: "success", nextCode: nextCode };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new salesman
function handleCreateSalesmanGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Description is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // If code is not provided, auto-generate it from the full Salesman sheet
  if (!code) {
    var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number
    for (var i = 1; i < salesmanData.length; i++) {
      if (salesmanData[i][0]) {
        var existingCode = salesmanData[i][0].toString();
        if (existingCode.toUpperCase().startsWith('SM')) {
          var num = parseInt(existingCode.substring(2));
          if (!isNaN(num) && num > maxNum) {
            maxNum = num;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    code = 'SM' + (maxNum + 1);
  }
  
  // Check if salesman code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < salesmanData.length; i++) {
    if (salesmanData[i][0] && salesmanData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Salesman code already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new salesman
  salesmanSheet.appendRow([code.toUpperCase(), description]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Salesman created successfully", code: code.toUpperCase() };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing salesman
function handleUpdateSalesmanGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Code and Description are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and update salesman
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < salesmanData.length; i++) {
    if (salesmanData[i][0] && salesmanData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      salesmanSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 2).setValues([[code.toUpperCase(), description]]);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Salesman not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Salesman updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete salesman
function handleDeleteSalesmanGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find the salesman first
  var found = false;
  var salesmanRowIndex = -1;
  for (var i = 1; i < salesmanData.length; i++) {
    if (salesmanData[i][0] && salesmanData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      salesmanRowIndex = i + 1;
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Salesman not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check for transaction/history references before deleting.
  // If references exist, block the delete to preserve financial/audit history.
  var refs = countSalesmanReferences(ss, code);
  var refParts = [];
  if (refs.salesHeaders > 0) refParts.push(refs.salesHeaders + (refs.salesHeaders === 1 ? " sale" : " sales"));
  if (refs.purchaseHeaders > 0) refParts.push(refs.purchaseHeaders + (refs.purchaseHeaders === 1 ? " purchase" : " purchases"));
  
  if (refParts.length > 0) {
    var msg = "Cannot delete: Salesman '" + code + "' is referenced in " + refParts.join(", ") + ". Delete or unlink these records first.";
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: msg };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // No references found - delete the salesman
  salesmanSheet.deleteRow(salesmanRowIndex);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Salesman deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Count references to a salesman code across transaction/history sheets
function countSalesmanReferences(ss, code) {
  var refs = {
    salesHeaders: 0,
    purchaseHeaders: 0
  };
  
  if (!code) return refs;
  
  var codeUpper = code.toString().toUpperCase();
  
  // SalesHeader sheet - salesman at index 18
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  if (salesHeaderSheet) {
    var salesData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < salesData.length; i++) {
      if (salesData[i][18] && salesData[i][18].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.salesHeaders++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // PurchaseHeader sheet - salesman at index 18
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  if (purchaseHeaderSheet) {
    var purchaseData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < purchaseData.length; i++) {
      if (purchaseData[i][18] && purchaseData[i][18].toString().toUpperCase() == codeUpper) {
        refs.purchaseHeaders++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return refs;
}

// ============================================
// SALES MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all sales
function handleGetSalesGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read pagination and search parameters
  var page = parseInt(e.parameter.page) || 1;
  var pageSize = parseInt(e.parameter.pageSize) || 10;
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all sales from SalesHeader sheet
  try {
    var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
    if (!salesHeaderSheet) {
      var emptyResult = { status: "success", sales: [], totalRecords: 0, totalPages: 0, currentPage: page, pageSize: pageSize };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
    }
    var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  } catch (e) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Error reading SalesHeader sheet: " + e.toString() };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var allSales = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      try {
        var docNo = salesHeaderData[i][1].toString();
        var customer = salesHeaderData[i][3] || '';
        var narration = salesHeaderData[i][4] || '';
        var date = '';
        if (salesHeaderData[i][2]) {
          try {
            var dateObj = new Date(salesHeaderData[i][2]);
            if (!isNaN(dateObj.getTime())) {
              date = Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
            } else {
              date = salesHeaderData[i][2].toString();
            }
          } catch (dateErr) {
            date = salesHeaderData[i][2].toString() || '';
          }
        }
        
        // Apply search filter if search term is provided
        if (search) {
          if (docNo.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
              customer.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
              narration.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
              date.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
            continue; // Skip this record if no match
          }
        }
        
        var refDate = '';
        if (salesHeaderData[i][17]) {
          try {
            var refDateObj = new Date(salesHeaderData[i][17]);
            if (!isNaN(refDateObj.getTime())) {
              refDate = Utilities.formatDate(refDateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
            } else {
              refDate = salesHeaderData[i][17].toString();
            }
          } catch (refDateErr) {
            refDate = salesHeaderData[i][17].toString() || '';
          }
        }
        
        allSales.push({
          docType: salesHeaderData[i][0] || '',
          docNo: docNo,
          date: date,
          customer: customer,
          salesman: salesHeaderData[i][18] || '',
          narration: narration,
          grossAmount: salesHeaderData[i][5] || 0,
          discountPercentage: salesHeaderData[i][6] || 0,
          discountAmount: salesHeaderData[i][7] || 0,
          vat: salesHeaderData[i][8] || 0,
          net: salesHeaderData[i][9] || 0,
          createdBy: salesHeaderData[i][10] || '',
          createdTime: salesHeaderData[i][11] || '',
          address: salesHeaderData[i][12] || '',
          trn: salesHeaderData[i][13] || '',
          vatTreatment: salesHeaderData[i][14] || '',
          rateType: salesHeaderData[i][15] || '',
          refNo: salesHeaderData[i][16] || '',
          refDate: refDate,
          postedBy: salesHeaderData[i][19] || '',
          postedTime: salesHeaderData[i][20] || ''
        });
      } catch (rowErr) {
        // Skip row that caused error and continue with next
        continue;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Sort descending by DocNo (most recent/last document first)
  allSales.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  var totalRecords = allSales.length;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize);
  
  // Get only the records for the requested page
  var startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalRecords);
  var sales = allSales.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    sales: sales, 
    totalRecords: totalRecords,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    currentPage: page,
    pageSize: pageSize
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get single sale by DocNo
function handleGetSaleByDocNoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get sale header
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: salesHeaderData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: salesHeaderData[i][1],
        date: salesHeaderData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(salesHeaderData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        customer: salesHeaderData[i][3] || '',
        salesman: salesHeaderData[i][18] || '',
        narration: salesHeaderData[i][4] || '',
        grossAmount: salesHeaderData[i][5] || 0,
        discountPercentage: salesHeaderData[i][6] || 0,
        discountAmount: salesHeaderData[i][7] || 0,
        vat: salesHeaderData[i][8] || 0,
        net: salesHeaderData[i][9] || 0,
        createdBy: salesHeaderData[i][10] || '',
        createdTime: salesHeaderData[i][11] || '',
        address: salesHeaderData[i][12] || '',
        trn: salesHeaderData[i][13] || '',
        vatTreatment: salesHeaderData[i][14] || '',
        rateType: salesHeaderData[i][15] || '',
        refNo: salesHeaderData[i][16] || '',
        refDate: salesHeaderData[i][17] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(salesHeaderData[i][17]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        postedBy: salesHeaderData[i][19] || '',
        postedTime: salesHeaderData[i][20] || ''
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Sale not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get sale details
  var salesDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
  var salesDetailsData = salesDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var details = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesDetailsData.length; i++) {
    if (salesDetailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      details.push({
        docType: salesDetailsData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: salesDetailsData[i][1],
        docSrNo: salesDetailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: salesDetailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: salesDetailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: salesDetailsData[i][5] || 0,
        unit: salesDetailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: salesDetailsData[i][7] || 0,
        grossAmount: salesDetailsData[i][8] || 0,
        discountAmount: salesDetailsData[i][9] || 0,
        discountPercentage: salesDetailsData[i][10] || 0,
        vatAmount: salesDetailsData[i][11] || 0,
        vatPercentage: salesDetailsData[i][12] || 0,
        netAmount: salesDetailsData[i][13] || 0,
        vatTreatment: salesDetailsData[i][14] || '',
        rateType: salesDetailsData[i][15] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", sale: { header: header, details: details } };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new sale
function handleCreateSaleGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var saleDataJson = e.parameter.saleData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse sale data
  var saleData;
  try {
    saleData = JSON.parse(saleDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid sale data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!saleData.header || !saleData.details || saleData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Generate DocNo
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1]) {
      var docNo = salesHeaderData[i][1].toString();
      if (docNo.startsWith('SI')) {
        var num = parseInt(docNo.substring(2));
        if (num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  var newDocNo = 'SI' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  // Add header
  var header = saleData.header;
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  salesHeaderSheet.appendRow([
    'SI', // DocType
    newDocNo,
    header.date,
    header.customer,
    header.narration || '',
    header.grossAmount || 0,
    header.discountPercentage || 0,
    header.discountAmount || 0,
    header.vat || 0,
    header.net || 0,
    validation.userName, // CreatedBy
    createdTime,
    header.address || '',
    header.trn || '',
    header.vatTreatment || '',
    header.rateType || '',
    header.refNo || '',
    header.refDate || '',
    header.salesman || ''
  ]);
  
  // Add details
  var salesDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
  for (var i = 0; i < saleData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = saleData.details[i];
    salesDetailsSheet.appendRow([
      'SI', // DocType
      newDocNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.grossAmount || 0,
      detail.discountAmount || 0,
      detail.discountPercentage || 0,
      detail.vatAmount || 0,
      detail.vatPercentage || 0,
      detail.netAmount || 0,
      detail.vatTreatment || '',
      detail.rateType || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Sale created successfully", docNo: newDocNo };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing sale
function handleUpdateSaleGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var saleDataJson = e.parameter.saleData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse sale data
  var saleData;
  try {
    saleData = JSON.parse(saleDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid sale data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!saleData.header || !saleData.header.docNo || !saleData.details || saleData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header with DocNo and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var docNo = saleData.header.docNo;
  
  // Update header
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1] == docNo) {
      var header = saleData.header;
      salesHeaderSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 19).setValues([[
        'SI', // DocType
        docNo,
        header.date,
        header.customer,
        header.narration || '',
        header.grossAmount || 0,
        header.discountPercentage || 0,
        header.discountAmount || 0,
        header.vat || 0,
        header.net || 0,
        salesHeaderData[i][10], // Keep original CreatedBy
        salesHeaderData[i][11], // Keep original CreatedTime
        header.address || '',
        header.trn || '',
        header.vatTreatment || '',
        header.rateType || '',
        header.refNo || '',
        header.refDate || '',
        header.salesman || ''
      ]]);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Sale not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete existing details
  var salesDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
  var salesDetailsData = salesDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  for (var i = salesDetailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (salesDetailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      salesDetailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new details
  for (var i = 0; i < saleData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = saleData.details[i];
    salesDetailsSheet.appendRow([
      'SI', // DocType
      docNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.grossAmount || 0,
      detail.discountAmount || 0,
      detail.discountPercentage || 0,
      detail.vatAmount || 0,
      detail.vatPercentage || 0,
      detail.netAmount || 0,
      detail.vatTreatment || '',
      detail.rateType || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Sale updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete sale
function handleDeleteSaleGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete header
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1] == docNo) {
      salesHeaderSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Sale not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete details
  var salesDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
  var salesDetailsData = salesDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  for (var i = salesDetailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (salesDetailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      salesDetailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Sale deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// PURCHASE MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all purchases
function handleGetPurchasesGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read pagination and search parameters
  var page = parseInt(e.parameter.page) || 1;
  var pageSize = parseInt(e.parameter.pageSize) || 10;
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all purchases from PurchaseHeader sheet
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  var purchaseHeaderData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allPurchases = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < purchaseHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (purchaseHeaderData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = purchaseHeaderData[i][1].toString();
      var supplier = purchaseHeaderData[i][3] || '';
      var narration = purchaseHeaderData[i][4] || '';
      var date = purchaseHeaderData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(purchaseHeaderData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply search filter if search term is provided
      if (search) {
        if (docNo.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            supplier.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            narration.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            date.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
          continue; // Skip this record if no match
        }
      }
      
      allPurchases.push({
        docType: purchaseHeaderData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: date,
        supplier: supplier,
        salesman: purchaseHeaderData[i][18] || '',
        narration: narration,
        grossAmount: purchaseHeaderData[i][5] || 0,
        discountPercentage: purchaseHeaderData[i][6] || 0,
        discountAmount: purchaseHeaderData[i][7] || 0,
        vat: purchaseHeaderData[i][8] || 0,
        net: purchaseHeaderData[i][9] || 0,
        createdBy: purchaseHeaderData[i][10] || '',
        createdTime: purchaseHeaderData[i][11] || '',
        address: purchaseHeaderData[i][12] || '',
        trn: purchaseHeaderData[i][13] || '',
        vatTreatment: purchaseHeaderData[i][14] || '',
        rateType: purchaseHeaderData[i][15] || '',
        refNo: purchaseHeaderData[i][16] || '',
        refDate: purchaseHeaderData[i][17] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(purchaseHeaderData[i][17]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        postedBy: purchaseHeaderData[i][19] || '',
        postedTime: purchaseHeaderData[i][20] || ''
      });

    }
  }
  
  // Sort descending by DocNo (most recent/last document first)
  allPurchases.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  var totalRecords = allPurchases.length;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize);
  
  // Get only the records for the requested page
  var startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalRecords);
  var purchases = allPurchases.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    purchases: purchases, 
    totalRecords: totalRecords,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    currentPage: page,
    pageSize: pageSize
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get single purchase by DocNo
function handleGetPurchaseByDocNoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get purchase header
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  var purchaseHeaderData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < purchaseHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (purchaseHeaderData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: purchaseHeaderData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: purchaseHeaderData[i][1],
        date: purchaseHeaderData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(purchaseHeaderData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        supplier: purchaseHeaderData[i][3] || '',
        salesman: purchaseHeaderData[i][18] || '',
        narration: purchaseHeaderData[i][4] || '',
        grossAmount: purchaseHeaderData[i][5] || 0,
        discountPercentage: purchaseHeaderData[i][6] || 0,
        discountAmount: purchaseHeaderData[i][7] || 0,
        vat: purchaseHeaderData[i][8] || 0,
        net: purchaseHeaderData[i][9] || 0,
        createdBy: purchaseHeaderData[i][10] || '',
        createdTime: purchaseHeaderData[i][11] || '',
        address: purchaseHeaderData[i][12] || '',
        trn: purchaseHeaderData[i][13] || '',
        vatTreatment: purchaseHeaderData[i][14] || '',
        rateType: purchaseHeaderData[i][15] || '',
        refNo: purchaseHeaderData[i][16] || '',
        refDate: purchaseHeaderData[i][17] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(purchaseHeaderData[i][17]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        postedBy: purchaseHeaderData[i][19] || '',
        postedTime: purchaseHeaderData[i][20] || ''
      };

      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Purchase not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get purchase details
  var purchaseDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
  var purchaseDetailsData = purchaseDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var details = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < purchaseDetailsData.length; i++) {
    if (purchaseDetailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      details.push({
        docType: purchaseDetailsData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: purchaseDetailsData[i][1],
        docSrNo: purchaseDetailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: purchaseDetailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: purchaseDetailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: purchaseDetailsData[i][5] || 0,
        unit: purchaseDetailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: purchaseDetailsData[i][7] || 0,
        grossAmount: purchaseDetailsData[i][8] || 0,
        discountAmount: purchaseDetailsData[i][9] || 0,
        discountPercentage: purchaseDetailsData[i][10] || 0,
        vatAmount: purchaseDetailsData[i][11] || 0,
        vatPercentage: purchaseDetailsData[i][12] || 0,
        netAmount: purchaseDetailsData[i][13] || 0,
        vatTreatment: purchaseDetailsData[i][14] || '',
        rateType: purchaseDetailsData[i][15] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", purchase: { header: header, details: details } };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new purchase
function handleCreatePurchaseGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var purchaseDataJson = e.parameter.purchaseData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse purchase data
  var purchaseData;
  try {
    purchaseData = JSON.parse(purchaseDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid purchase data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!purchaseData.header || !purchaseData.details || purchaseData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Generate DocNo
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  var purchaseHeaderData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number
  
  for (var i = 1; i < purchaseHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (purchaseHeaderData[i][1]) {
      var docNo = purchaseHeaderData[i][1].toString();
      if (docNo.startsWith('PI')) {
        var num = parseInt(docNo.substring(2));
        if (num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  var newDocNo = 'PI' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  // Add header
  var header = purchaseData.header;
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  purchaseHeaderSheet.appendRow([
    'PI', // DocType
    newDocNo,
    header.date,
    header.supplier,
    header.narration || '',
    header.grossAmount || 0,
    header.discountPercentage || 0,
    header.discountAmount || 0,
    header.vat || 0,
    header.net || 0,
    validation.userName, // CreatedBy
    createdTime,
    header.address || '',
    header.trn || '',
    header.vatTreatment || '',
    header.rateType || '',
    header.refNo || '',
    header.refDate || '',
    header.salesman || ''
  ]);
  
  // Add details
  var purchaseDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
  for (var i = 0; i < purchaseData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = purchaseData.details[i];
    purchaseDetailsSheet.appendRow([
      'PI', // DocType
      newDocNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.grossAmount || 0,
      detail.discountAmount || 0,
      detail.discountPercentage || 0,
      detail.vatAmount || 0,
      detail.vatPercentage || 0,
      detail.netAmount || 0,
      detail.vatTreatment || '',
      detail.rateType || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Purchase created successfully", docNo: newDocNo };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing purchase
function handleUpdatePurchaseGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var purchaseDataJson = e.parameter.purchaseData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse purchase data
  var purchaseData;
  try {
    purchaseData = JSON.parse(purchaseDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid purchase data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!purchaseData.header || !purchaseData.header.docNo || !purchaseData.details || purchaseData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header with DocNo and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var docNo = purchaseData.header.docNo;
  
  // Update header
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  var purchaseHeaderData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < purchaseHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (purchaseHeaderData[i][1] == docNo) {
      var header = purchaseData.header;
      purchaseHeaderSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 19).setValues([[
        'PI', // DocType
        docNo,
        header.date,
        header.supplier,
        header.narration || '',
        header.grossAmount || 0,
        header.discountPercentage || 0,
        header.discountAmount || 0,
        header.vat || 0,
        header.net || 0,
        purchaseHeaderData[i][10], // Keep original CreatedBy
        purchaseHeaderData[i][11], // Keep original CreatedTime
        header.address || '',
        header.trn || '',
        header.vatTreatment || '',
        header.rateType || '',
        header.refNo || '',
        header.refDate || '',
        header.salesman || ''
      ]]);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Purchase not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete existing details
  var purchaseDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
  var purchaseDetailsData = purchaseDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  for (var i = purchaseDetailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (purchaseDetailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      purchaseDetailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new details
  for (var i = 0; i < purchaseData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = purchaseData.details[i];
    purchaseDetailsSheet.appendRow([
      'PI', // DocType
      docNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.grossAmount || 0,
      detail.discountAmount || 0,
      detail.discountPercentage || 0,
      detail.vatAmount || 0,
      detail.vatPercentage || 0,
      detail.netAmount || 0,
      detail.vatTreatment || '',
      detail.rateType || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Purchase updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete purchase
function handleDeletePurchaseGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete header
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  var purchaseHeaderData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < purchaseHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (purchaseHeaderData[i][1] == docNo) {
      purchaseHeaderSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Purchase not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete details
  var purchaseDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
  var purchaseDetailsData = purchaseDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  for (var i = purchaseDetailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (purchaseDetailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      purchaseDetailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Purchase deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// LOGO MASTER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all logos
function handleGetLogosGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all logos from LogoMaster sheet
  var logoSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogoMaster");
  if (!logoSheet) {
    // Create sheet if it doesn't exist
    logoSheet = ss.insertSheet("LogoMaster");
    logoSheet.appendRow(["Code", "Description", "LogoPath", "LogoData"]);
  }
  
  var logoData = logoSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var logos = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < logoData.length; i++) {
    if (logoData[i][0]) { // Check if Code exists
      logos.push({
        code: logoData[i][0],
        description: logoData[i][1] || '',
        logoPath: logoData[i][2] || '',
        logoData: logoData[i][3] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", logos: logos };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Upload logo file - stores base64 data directly in the sheet (no Drive needed)
function handleUploadLogoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var base64Data = e.parameter.base64Data;
  var fileName = e.parameter.fileName;
  var mimeType = e.parameter.mimeType;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!base64Data || !fileName) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "File data and name are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  try {
    // Store the base64 data directly - no Drive upload needed
    // The data will be saved to the sheet when createLogo or updateLogo is called
    var dataUrl = "data:" + (mimeType || 'image/png') + ";base64," + base64Data;
    
    var result = { status: "success", message: "Logo processed successfully", fileUrl: dataUrl };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(result);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Error processing logo: " + error.toString() };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
}

// POST version of upload logo (handles large files)
function handleUploadLogoPost(requestData, e) {
  var sessionId = requestData.sessionId;
  var userId = requestData.userId;
  var base64Data = requestData.base64Data;
  var fileName = requestData.fileName;
  var mimeType = requestData.mimeType;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" });
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!base64Data || !fileName) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "File data and name are required" });
  }
  
  try {
    // Store the base64 data directly - no Drive upload needed
    var dataUrl = "data:" + (mimeType || 'image/png') + ";base64," + base64Data;
    
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "success", message: "Logo processed successfully", fileUrl: dataUrl });
  } catch (error) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Error processing logo: " + error.toString() });
  }
}

// POST version of create logo (handles large base64 data)
function handleCreateLogoPost(requestData, e) {
  var sessionId = requestData.sessionId;
  var userId = requestData.userId;
  var code = requestData.code;
  var description = requestData.description;
  var logoPath = requestData.logoPath || '';
  var logoData = requestData.logoData || '';
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" });
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !description) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Code and Description are required" });
  }
  
  var logoSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogoMaster");
  if (!logoSheet) {
    logoSheet = ss.insertSheet("LogoMaster");
    logoSheet.appendRow(["Code", "Description", "LogoPath", "LogoData"]);
  }
  
  var existingData = logoSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Check if logo code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < existingData.length; i++) {
    if (existingData[i][0] && existingData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Logo code already exists" });
    }
  }
  
  // Add new logo with base64 data
  logoSheet.appendRow([code.toUpperCase(), description, logoPath, logoData]);
  
  return createJsonResponse({ status: "success", message: "Logo created successfully", code: code.toUpperCase() });
}

// POST version of update logo (handles large base64 data)
function handleUpdateLogoPost(requestData, e) {
  var sessionId = requestData.sessionId;
  var userId = requestData.userId;
  var code = requestData.code;
  var description = requestData.description;
  var logoPath = requestData.logoPath || '';
  var logoData = requestData.logoData || '';
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" });
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !description) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Code and Description are required" });
  }
  
  var logoSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogoMaster");
  if (!logoSheet) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "LogoMaster sheet not found" });
  }
  
  var existingData = logoSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and update logo
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < existingData.length; i++) {
    if (existingData[i][0] && existingData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      logoSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([[code.toUpperCase(), description, logoPath, logoData]]);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    return createJsonResponse({ status: "error", message: "Logo not found" });
  }
  
  return createJsonResponse({ status: "success", message: "Logo updated successfully" });
}

// Create new logo
function handleCreateLogoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var logoPath = e.parameter.logoPath || '';
  var logoData = e.parameter.logoData || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Code and Description are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var logoSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogoMaster");
  if (!logoSheet) {
    logoSheet = ss.insertSheet("LogoMaster");
    logoSheet.appendRow(["Code", "Description", "LogoPath", "LogoData"]);
  }
  
  var existingData = logoSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Check if logo code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < existingData.length; i++) {
    if (existingData[i][0] && existingData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Logo code already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new logo with base64 data
  logoSheet.appendRow([code.toUpperCase(), description, logoPath, logoData]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Logo created successfully", code: code.toUpperCase() };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
