            debit: amount < 0 ? Math.abs(amount) : 0,
            credit: amount > 0 ? amount : 0,
            narration: detailsData[d][7] || '',
            mode: detailsData[d][8] || ''
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Attach details to each voucher
      for (var vi = 0; vi < allVouchers.length; vi++) {
        allVouchers[vi].details = detailsMap[allVouchers[vi].docNo] || [];
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    jv: allVouchers,
    reportType: reportType,
    summary: {
      totalCount: allVouchers.length,
      totalDebit: Math.round(totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(totalCredit * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// BANK PAYMENT VOUCHER (BPV) MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all bank payment vouchers (with server-side pagination)
function handleGetBankPaymentVouchersGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read pagination and search parameters
  var page = parseInt(e.parameter.page) || 1;
  var pageSize = parseInt(e.parameter.pageSize) || 10;
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all vouchers from JournalVoucherHeader sheet (shared with JV)
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", jv: [], totalRecords: 0, totalPages: 0, currentPage: page, pageSize: pageSize };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allVouchers = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    // Only include Bank Payment Voucher (BPV) documents - shared sheet with JV
    if (String(headerData[i][0] || '').toUpperCase() !== 'BPV') continue;
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var narration = headerData[i][3] || '';
      var date = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply search filter if search term is provided
      if (search) {
        if (docNo.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            narration.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            date.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
          continue;
        }
      }
      
      allVouchers.push({
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: date,
        narration: narration,
        debit: parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0,
        credit: parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort descending by DocNo (most recent/last document first)
  allVouchers.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  var totalRecords = allVouchers.length;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize);
  
  var startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalRecords);
  var vouchers = allVouchers.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    jv: vouchers, 
    totalRecords: totalRecords,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    currentPage: page,
    pageSize: pageSize
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get single bank payment voucher by DocNo
function handleGetBankPaymentVoucherByDocNoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Bank payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: headerData[i][1],
        date: headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        narration: headerData[i][3] || '',
        debit: parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0,
        credit: parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Bank payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet ? detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var details = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      details.push({
        docType: detailsData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: detailsData[i][1],
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        acCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        acDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        subledger: detailsData[i][5] || '',
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][6]) || 0,
        narration: detailsData[i][7] || '',
        mode: detailsData[i][8] || '',
        bank: detailsData[i][9] || '',
        instrumentNo: detailsData[i][10] || '',
        instrumentDate: detailsData[i][11] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(detailsData[i][11]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", jv: { header: header, details: details } };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new bank payment voucher
function handleCreateBankPaymentVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var bpvDataJson = e.parameter.bpvData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse bank payment voucher data
  var bpvData;
  try {
    bpvData = JSON.parse(bpvDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid bank payment voucher data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!bpvData.header || !bpvData.details || bpvData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate debit = credit balance
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  for (var d = 0; d < bpvData.details.length; d++) {
    var amt = parseFloat(bpvData.details[d].amount) || 0;
    if (amt < 0) {
      totalDebit += Math.abs(amt);
    } else {
      totalCredit += amt;
    }
  }
  
  if (Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) > 0.01) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Voucher is not balanced! Total Debit must equal Total Credit." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get or create JournalVoucherHeader sheet (shared with JV)
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    headerSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherHeader");
    headerSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "Date", "Narration", "Debit", "Credit", "RefNo", "RefDate", "CreatedBy", "CreatedTime", "PostedBy", "PostedTime"]);
  }
  
  // Generate DocNo - starting from 1000 per sample
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 1000; // Starting number
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) {
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      if (docNo.startsWith('BPV')) {
        var num = parseInt(docNo.substring(3));
        if (num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Validate the pending allocations (allocation is allowed only for accounts
  // that have a subledger of type AR/AP)
  var allocValidation = validateAllocationSet(ss, bpvData.details);
  if (!allocValidation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: allocValidation.message };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var newDocNo = 'BPV' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  // Add header
  var header = bpvData.header;
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  headerSheet.appendRow([
    'BPV', // DocType
    newDocNo,
    header.date,
    header.narration || '',
    totalDebit,
    totalCredit,
    header.refNo || '',
    header.refDate || '',
    validation.userName,
    createdTime,
    '', // PostedBy
    ''  // PostedTime
  ]);
  
  // Get or create JournalVoucherDetails sheet (shared with JV)
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "ACCode", "ACDescription", "Subledger", "Amount", "Narration", "Mode", "Bank", "InstrumentNo", "InstrumentDate"]);
  }
  
  // Add details
  for (var i = 0; i < bpvData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = bpvData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'BPV', // DocType
      newDocNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.acCode,
      detail.acDescription || '',
      detail.subledger || '',
      parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0,
      detail.narration || '',
      detail.mode || '',
      detail.bank || '',
      detail.instrumentNo || '',
      detail.instrumentDate || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  // Save the pending allocations of the voucher
  saveAllocationDraftsToSheet(ss, 'BPV', newDocNo, bpvData.details);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Bank Payment Voucher created successfully", docNo: newDocNo };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// Update existing bank payment voucher
function handleUpdateBankPaymentVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var bpvDataJson = e.parameter.bpvData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse bank payment voucher data
  var bpvData;
  try {
    bpvData = JSON.parse(bpvDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid bank payment voucher data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!bpvData.header || !bpvData.header.docNo || !bpvData.details || bpvData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header with DocNo and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var docNo = bpvData.header.docNo;
  
  // Validate the pending allocations before changing anything
  var allocValidation = validateAllocationSet(ss, bpvData.details);
  if (!allocValidation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: allocValidation.message };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate debit = credit balance
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  for (var d = 0; d < bpvData.details.length; d++) {
    var amt = parseFloat(bpvData.details[d].amount) || 0;
    if (amt < 0) {
      totalDebit += Math.abs(amt);
    } else {
      totalCredit += amt;
    }
  }
  
  if (Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) > 0.01) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Voucher is not balanced! Total Debit must equal Total Credit." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Update header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      // Check if posted
      if (headerData[i][10] && headerData[i][10] !== '') {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cannot update a posted bank payment voucher. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
      
      var header = bpvData.header;
      headerSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 12).setValues([[
        'BPV', // DocType
        docNo,
        header.date,
        header.narration || '',
        totalDebit,
        totalCredit,
        header.refNo || '',
        header.refDate || '',
        headerData[i][8], // Keep original CreatedBy
        headerData[i][9], // Keep original CreatedTime
        '', // PostedBy
        ''  // PostedTime
      ]]);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Bank payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete existing details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  } else {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "ACCode", "ACDescription", "Subledger", "Amount", "Narration", "Mode", "Bank", "InstrumentNo", "InstrumentDate"]);
  }
  
  // Add new details
  for (var i = 0; i < bpvData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = bpvData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'BPV', // DocType
      docNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.acCode,
      detail.acDescription || '',
      detail.subledger || '',
      parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0,
      detail.narration || '',
      detail.mode || '',
      detail.bank || '',
      detail.instrumentNo || '',
      detail.instrumentDate || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  // Save the pending allocations of the voucher
  saveAllocationDraftsToSheet(ss, 'BPV', docNo, bpvData.details);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Bank Payment Voucher updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete bank payment voucher
function handleDeleteBankPaymentVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      // Check if posted
      if (headerData[i][10] && headerData[i][10] !== '') {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cannot delete a posted bank payment voucher. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
      headerSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Bank payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Bank Payment Voucher deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Post BPV to AccountTransaction
function handlePostBPVToAccountTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var headerDate = '';
  var postedBy = '';
  var headerRefNo = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      headerDate = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      postedBy = headerData[i][10] || '';
      headerRefNo = headerData[i][6] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Bank payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Bank payment voucher is already posted by " + postedBy };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if accounting entries already exist
  var existingSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (existingSheet) {
    var existingData = existingSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < existingData.length; i++) {
      if (existingData[i][0] && existingData[i][0].toString() == 'BPV' && existingData[i][1] && existingData[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
        var alreadyResult = { status: "error", message: "Accounting entries already exist for this document. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(alreadyResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(alreadyResult);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get voucher details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var bpvDetails = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      bpvDetails.push({
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        acCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        subledger: detailsData[i][5] || '',
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][6]) || 0,
        narration: detailsData[i][7] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  if (bpvDetails.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No details found for this document" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists
  var accountTransactionSheet = ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  var now = new Date();
  var postedTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  // Validate allocations before posting (allocation is only possible for
  // accounts that have a subledger and the invoice balances must cover them)
  var allocValidation = validateAllocationSet(ss, getAllocationDraftsForVoucher(ss, 'BPV', docNo));
  if (!allocValidation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: allocValidation.message };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Post each detail line to AccountTransaction
  for (var d = 0; d < bpvDetails.length; d++) {
    var detail = bpvDetails[d];
    var amount = detail.amount;
    
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'BPV',                   // DOCTYPE
      docNo,                   // DOCNO
      detail.docSrNo,          // DOCSRNO
      detail.acCode,           // AC_DOCNO
      detail.subledger,        // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
      headerDate,              // DOCDATE
      headerDate,              // VALDATE
      detail.narration || 'Bank Payment Voucher ' + docNo, // NARRATION
      amount,                  // AMOUNT (negative = debit, positive = credit)
      amount,                  // BAL_AMOUNT
      headerRefNo,             // REF_NO
      headerDate,              // REF_DATE
      validation.userName,     // CREATED_BY
      postedTime               // CREATED_TS
    ]);
  }
  
  // Update JournalVoucherHeader with PostedBy and PostedTime
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 11).setValue(validation.userName);
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 12).setValue(postedTime);
  
  // Apply the allocations: write them to the Allocation sheet and update the
  // BAL_AMOUNT of both the voucher line and the allocated invoice line
  var allocApplied = applyVoucherAllocations(ss, 'BPV', docNo);
  if (!allocApplied.success) {
    var allocError = { status: "error", message: "Voucher posted but allocation failed: " + allocApplied.message + " Please cancel the post, correct the allocation and post again." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(allocError) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(allocError);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Bank Payment Voucher posted to accounting successfully", postedBy: validation.userName, postedTime: postedTime, allocatedRows: allocApplied.rows, allocatedAmount: allocApplied.amount };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel BPV AccountTransaction Post (Delete entries)
function handleCancelBPVAccountTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Find header row for clearing posted status
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerRow = -1;
  if (headerSheet) {
    var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
      if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
        headerRow = i;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Delete from AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var deleted = false;
    
    // Reverse the allocations of this voucher (delete its Allocation rows and
    // restore the invoice balances)
    reverseVoucherAllocations(ss, 'BPV', docNo);
    
    for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (data[i][0] && data[i][0].toString() == 'BPV' && data[i][1] && data[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
        accountTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        deleted = true;
      }
    }
    
    // Clear PostedBy and PostedTime in JournalVoucherHeader
    if (headerRow !== -1 && deleted) {
      headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 11).setValue('');
      headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 12).setValue('');
    }
    
    var result = { status: "success", message: deleted ? "Accounting entries cancelled successfully" : "No accounting entries found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(result);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "No accounting entries found" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// ============================================
// BANK PAYMENT VOUCHER REPORT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get bank payment voucher report with date range filters
function handleGetBankPaymentVoucherReportGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read filter parameters
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var reportType = (e.parameter.reportType || 'headerwise').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all bank payment vouchers from JournalVoucherHeader sheet (shared with JV)
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", jv: [], summary: { totalCount: 0, totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 } };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allVouchers = [];
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    // Only include Bank Payment Voucher (BPV) documents - shared sheet with JV
    if (String(headerData[i][0] || '').toUpperCase() !== 'BPV') continue;
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var dateObj = headerData[i][2] ? new Date(headerData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply date range filter
      if (fromDate && dateStr < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      var debit = parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0;
      var credit = parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0;
      totalDebit += debit;
      totalCredit += credit;
      
      var voucher = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: dateStr,
        narration: headerData[i][3] || '',
        debit: debit,
        credit: credit,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      };
      
      allVouchers.push(voucher);
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date descending, then by DocNo descending
  allVouchers.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return b.date.localeCompare(a.date);
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  // If reportType is linewise, fetch detail lines for each voucher
  if (reportType === 'linewise') {
    var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
    if (detailsSheet) {
      var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      
      // Build a map of docNo -> details array
      var detailsMap = {};
      for (var d = 1; d < detailsData.length; d++) {
        var detailDocNo = detailsData[d][1];
        if (detailDocNo) {
          if (!detailsMap[detailDocNo]) {
            detailsMap[detailDocNo] = [];
          }
          var amount = parseFloat(detailsData[d][6]) || 0;
          detailsMap[detailDocNo].push({
            docSrNo: detailsData[d][2] || 0,
            acCode: detailsData[d][3] || '',
            acDescription: detailsData[d][4] || '',
            subledger: detailsData[d][5] || '',
            amount: amount,
            debit: amount < 0 ? Math.abs(amount) : 0,
            credit: amount > 0 ? amount : 0,
            narration: detailsData[d][7] || '',
            mode: detailsData[d][8] || ''
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Attach details to each voucher
      for (var vi = 0; vi < allVouchers.length; vi++) {
        allVouchers[vi].details = detailsMap[allVouchers[vi].docNo] || [];
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    jv: allVouchers,
    reportType: reportType,
    summary: {
      totalCount: allVouchers.length,
      totalDebit: Math.round(totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(totalCredit * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// CASH PAYMENT VOUCHER (CPV) MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all cash payment vouchers (with server-side pagination)
function handleGetCashPaymentVouchersGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read pagination and search parameters
  var page = parseInt(e.parameter.page) || 1;
  var pageSize = parseInt(e.parameter.pageSize) || 10;
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all vouchers from JournalVoucherHeader sheet (shared with JV)
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", jv: [], totalRecords: 0, totalPages: 0, currentPage: page, pageSize: pageSize };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allVouchers = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    // Only include Cash Payment Voucher (CPV) documents - shared sheet with JV
    if (String(headerData[i][0] || '').toUpperCase() !== 'CPV') continue;
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var narration = headerData[i][3] || '';
      var date = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply search filter if search term is provided
      if (search) {
        if (docNo.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            narration.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            date.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
          continue;
        }
      }
      
      allVouchers.push({
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: date,
        narration: narration,
        debit: parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0,
        credit: parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort descending by DocNo (most recent/last document first)
  allVouchers.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  var totalRecords = allVouchers.length;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize);
  
  var startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalRecords);
  var vouchers = allVouchers.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    jv: vouchers, 
    totalRecords: totalRecords,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    currentPage: page,
    pageSize: pageSize
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get single cash payment voucher by DocNo
function handleGetCashPaymentVoucherByDocNoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cash payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: headerData[i][1],
        date: headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        narration: headerData[i][3] || '',
        debit: parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0,
        credit: parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cash payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet ? detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var details = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      details.push({
        docType: detailsData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: detailsData[i][1],
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        acCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        acDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        subledger: detailsData[i][5] || '',
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][6]) || 0,
        narration: detailsData[i][7] || '',
        mode: detailsData[i][8] || '',
        bank: detailsData[i][9] || '',
        instrumentNo: detailsData[i][10] || '',
        instrumentDate: detailsData[i][11] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(detailsData[i][11]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", jv: { header: header, details: details } };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new cash payment voucher
function handleCreateCashPaymentVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var cpvDataJson = e.parameter.cpvData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse cash payment voucher data
  var cpvData;
  try {
    cpvData = JSON.parse(cpvDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid cash payment voucher data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!cpvData.header || !cpvData.details || cpvData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate debit = credit balance
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  for (var d = 0; d < cpvData.details.length; d++) {
    var amt = parseFloat(cpvData.details[d].amount) || 0;
    if (amt < 0) {
      totalDebit += Math.abs(amt);
    } else {
      totalCredit += amt;
    }
  }
  
  if (Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) > 0.01) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Voucher is not balanced! Total Debit must equal Total Credit." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get or create JournalVoucherHeader sheet (shared with JV)
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    headerSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherHeader");
    headerSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "Date", "Narration", "Debit", "Credit", "RefNo", "RefDate", "CreatedBy", "CreatedTime", "PostedBy", "PostedTime"]);
  }
  
  // Generate DocNo - starting from 1000 per sample
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 1000; // Starting number
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) {
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      if (docNo.startsWith('CPV')) {
        var num = parseInt(docNo.substring(3));
        if (num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Validate the pending allocations (allocation is allowed only for accounts
  // that have a subledger of type AR/AP)
  var allocValidation = validateAllocationSet(ss, cpvData.details);
  if (!allocValidation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: allocValidation.message };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var newDocNo = 'CPV' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  // Add header
  var header = cpvData.header;
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  headerSheet.appendRow([
    'CPV', // DocType
    newDocNo,
    header.date,
    header.narration || '',
    totalDebit,
    totalCredit,
    header.refNo || '',
    header.refDate || '',
    validation.userName,
    createdTime,
    '', // PostedBy
    ''  // PostedTime
  ]);
  
  // Get or create JournalVoucherDetails sheet (shared with JV)
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "ACCode", "ACDescription", "Subledger", "Amount", "Narration", "Mode", "Bank", "InstrumentNo", "InstrumentDate"]);
  }
  
  // Add details
  for (var i = 0; i < cpvData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = cpvData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'CPV', // DocType
      newDocNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.acCode,
      detail.acDescription || '',
      detail.subledger || '',
      parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0,
      detail.narration || '',
      detail.mode || '',
      detail.bank || '',
      detail.instrumentNo || '',
      detail.instrumentDate || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  // Save the pending allocations of the voucher
  saveAllocationDraftsToSheet(ss, 'CPV', newDocNo, cpvData.details);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Cash Payment Voucher created successfully", docNo: newDocNo };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// Update existing cash payment voucher
function handleUpdateCashPaymentVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var cpvDataJson = e.parameter.cpvData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse cash payment voucher data
  var cpvData;
  try {
    cpvData = JSON.parse(cpvDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid cash payment voucher data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!cpvData.header || !cpvData.header.docNo || !cpvData.details || cpvData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header with DocNo and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var docNo = cpvData.header.docNo;
  
  // Validate the pending allocations before changing anything
  var allocValidation = validateAllocationSet(ss, cpvData.details);
  if (!allocValidation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: allocValidation.message };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate debit = credit balance
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  for (var d = 0; d < cpvData.details.length; d++) {
    var amt = parseFloat(cpvData.details[d].amount) || 0;
    if (amt < 0) {
      totalDebit += Math.abs(amt);
    } else {
      totalCredit += amt;
    }
  }
  
  if (Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) > 0.01) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Voucher is not balanced! Total Debit must equal Total Credit." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Update header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      // Check if posted
      if (headerData[i][10] && headerData[i][10] !== '') {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cannot update a posted cash payment voucher. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
      
      var header = cpvData.header;
      headerSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 12).setValues([[
        'CPV', // DocType
        docNo,
        header.date,
        header.narration || '',
        totalDebit,
        totalCredit,
        header.refNo || '',
        header.refDate || '',
        headerData[i][8], // Keep original CreatedBy
        headerData[i][9], // Keep original CreatedTime
        '', // PostedBy
        ''  // PostedTime
      ]]);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cash payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete existing details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  } else {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "ACCode", "ACDescription", "Subledger", "Amount", "Narration", "Mode", "Bank", "InstrumentNo", "InstrumentDate"]);
  }
  
  // Add new details
  for (var i = 0; i < cpvData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = cpvData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'CPV', // DocType
      docNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.acCode,
      detail.acDescription || '',
      detail.subledger || '',
      parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0,
      detail.narration || '',
      detail.mode || '',
      detail.bank || '',
      detail.instrumentNo || '',
      detail.instrumentDate || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  // Save the pending allocations of the voucher
  saveAllocationDraftsToSheet(ss, 'CPV', docNo, cpvData.details);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Cash Payment Voucher updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete cash payment voucher
function handleDeleteCashPaymentVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      // Check if posted
      if (headerData[i][10] && headerData[i][10] !== '') {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cannot delete a posted cash payment voucher. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
      headerSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cash payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Cash Payment Voucher deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Post CPV to AccountTransaction
function handlePostCPVToAccountTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var headerDate = '';
  var postedBy = '';
  var headerRefNo = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      headerDate = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      postedBy = headerData[i][10] || '';
      headerRefNo = headerData[i][6] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cash payment voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cash payment voucher is already posted by " + postedBy };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if accounting entries already exist
  var existingSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (existingSheet) {
    var existingData = existingSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < existingData.length; i++) {
      if (existingData[i][0] && existingData[i][0].toString() == 'CPV' && existingData[i][1] && existingData[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
        var alreadyResult = { status: "error", message: "Accounting entries already exist for this document. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(alreadyResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(alreadyResult);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get voucher details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var cpvDetails = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      cpvDetails.push({
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        acCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        subledger: detailsData[i][5] || '',
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][6]) || 0,
        narration: detailsData[i][7] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  if (cpvDetails.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No details found for this document" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists
  var accountTransactionSheet = ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  var now = new Date();
  var postedTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  // Validate allocations before posting (allocation is only possible for
  // accounts that have a subledger and the invoice balances must cover them)
  var allocValidation = validateAllocationSet(ss, getAllocationDraftsForVoucher(ss, 'CPV', docNo));
  if (!allocValidation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: allocValidation.message };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Post each detail line to AccountTransaction
  for (var d = 0; d < cpvDetails.length; d++) {
    var detail = cpvDetails[d];
    var amount = detail.amount;
    
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'CPV',                   // DOCTYPE
      docNo,                   // DOCNO
      detail.docSrNo,          // DOCSRNO
      detail.acCode,           // AC_DOCNO
      detail.subledger,        // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
      headerDate,              // DOCDATE
      headerDate,              // VALDATE
      detail.narration || 'Cash Payment Voucher ' + docNo, // NARRATION
      amount,                  // AMOUNT (negative = debit, positive = credit)
      amount,                  // BAL_AMOUNT
      headerRefNo,             // REF_NO
      headerDate,              // REF_DATE
      validation.userName,     // CREATED_BY
      postedTime               // CREATED_TS
    ]);
  }
  
  // Update JournalVoucherHeader with PostedBy and PostedTime
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 11).setValue(validation.userName);
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 12).setValue(postedTime);
  
  // Apply the allocations: write them to the Allocation sheet and update the
  // BAL_AMOUNT of both the voucher line and the allocated invoice line
  var allocApplied = applyVoucherAllocations(ss, 'CPV', docNo);
  if (!allocApplied.success) {
    var allocError = { status: "error", message: "Voucher posted but allocation failed: " + allocApplied.message + " Please cancel the post, correct the allocation and post again." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(allocError) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(allocError);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Cash Payment Voucher posted to accounting successfully", postedBy: validation.userName, postedTime: postedTime, allocatedRows: allocApplied.rows, allocatedAmount: allocApplied.amount };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel CPV AccountTransaction Post (Delete entries)
function handleCancelCPVAccountTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Find header row for clearing posted status
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerRow = -1;
  if (headerSheet) {
    var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
      if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
        headerRow = i;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Delete from AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var deleted = false;
    
    // Reverse the allocations of this voucher (delete its Allocation rows and
    // restore the invoice balances)
    reverseVoucherAllocations(ss, 'CPV', docNo);
    
    for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (data[i][0] && data[i][0].toString() == 'CPV' && data[i][1] && data[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
        accountTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        deleted = true;
      }
    }
    
    // Clear PostedBy and PostedTime in JournalVoucherHeader
    if (headerRow !== -1 && deleted) {
      headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 11).setValue('');
      headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 12).setValue('');
    }
    
    var result = { status: "success", message: deleted ? "Accounting entries cancelled successfully" : "No accounting entries found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(result);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "No accounting entries found" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// ============================================
// CASH PAYMENT VOUCHER REPORT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get cash payment voucher report with date range filters
function handleGetCashPaymentVoucherReportGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read filter parameters
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var reportType = (e.parameter.reportType || 'headerwise').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all cash payment vouchers from JournalVoucherHeader sheet (shared with JV)
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", jv: [], summary: { totalCount: 0, totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 } };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allVouchers = [];
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    // Only include Cash Payment Voucher (CPV) documents - shared sheet with JV
    if (String(headerData[i][0] || '').toUpperCase() !== 'CPV') continue;
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var dateObj = headerData[i][2] ? new Date(headerData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply date range filter
      if (fromDate && dateStr < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      var debit = parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0;
      var credit = parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0;
      totalDebit += debit;
      totalCredit += credit;
      
      var voucher = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: dateStr,
        narration: headerData[i][3] || '',
        debit: debit,
        credit: credit,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      };
      
      allVouchers.push(voucher);
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date descending, then by DocNo descending
  allVouchers.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return b.date.localeCompare(a.date);
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  // If reportType is linewise, fetch detail lines for each voucher
  if (reportType === 'linewise') {
    var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
    if (detailsSheet) {
      var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      
      // Build a map of docNo -> details array
      var detailsMap = {};
      for (var d = 1; d < detailsData.length; d++) {
        var detailDocNo = detailsData[d][1];
        if (detailDocNo) {
          if (!detailsMap[detailDocNo]) {
            detailsMap[detailDocNo] = [];
          }
          var amount = parseFloat(detailsData[d][6]) || 0;
          detailsMap[detailDocNo].push({
            docSrNo: detailsData[d][2] || 0,
            acCode: detailsData[d][3] || '',
            acDescription: detailsData[d][4] || '',
            subledger: detailsData[d][5] || '',
            amount: amount,
            debit: amount < 0 ? Math.abs(amount) : 0,
            credit: amount > 0 ? amount : 0,
            narration: detailsData[d][7] || '',
            mode: detailsData[d][8] || ''
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Attach details to each voucher
      for (var vi = 0; vi < allVouchers.length; vi++) {
        allVouchers[vi].details = detailsMap[allVouchers[vi].docNo] || [];
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    jv: allVouchers,
    reportType: reportType,
    summary: {
      totalCount: allVouchers.length,
      totalDebit: Math.round(totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(totalCredit * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// ============================================
// DOCUMENT ALLOCATION (AR / AP) HANDLERS
// ============================================
// Allocation is possible only for accounts that have a subledger
// (COA SUBLEDGER_EXISTS = 1) whose subledger type (COA ACTYPE_DOCNO) is:
//   AR -> pending Sales Invoices (SI)    for the same account + customer
//   AP -> pending Purchase Invoices (PI) for the same account + supplier
// The voucher (From) keeps its unallocated amount and the invoice (To) keeps
// its unsettled amount in AccountTransaction.BAL_AMOUNT:
//   From line : BAL_AMOUNT += Amount   (AR lines are negative, AP are positive)
//   To line   : BAL_AMOUNT -= Amount
// Allocation rows are written to the "Allocation" sheet when the voucher is
// posted, and removed again when the post is cancelled.
// Pending allocations are kept in JournalVoucherDetails.AllocationJSON (col M).

var ALLOCATION_COLUMN = 13; // JournalVoucherDetails column M = AllocationJSON
var ALLOCATION_HEADERS = ["FromDocType", "FromDocNo", "FromDocSrNo", "FromDocSrSrNo", "AccountType", "SubledgerDocNo",
                          "ToDocType", "ToDocNo", "ToDocSrNo", "ToDocSrSrNo", "Amount"];

// Ensure Allocation sheet exists with proper headers
function ensureAllocationSheet(ss) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Allocation");
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("Allocation");
    sheet.appendRow(ALLOCATION_HEADERS);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Ensure JournalVoucherDetails has the AllocationJSON column (column M)
function ensureDetailAllocationColumn(ss, detailsSheet) {
  if (!detailsSheet) return;
  var headerCell = detailsSheet.getRange(1, ALLOCATION_COLUMN);
  if (String(headerCell.getValue() || '') !== 'AllocationJSON') {
    headerCell.setValue('AllocationJSON');
  }
}

// Normalize a code (account, subledger, document type/number) for comparisons.
// Handles stray spaces and letter case from manual entry in the sheet.
function normalizeAllocationCode(value) {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return '';
  return String(value).replace(/\s+/g, '').toUpperCase();
}

// Key of one ledger document line, used by every allocation lookup
function allocationDocKey(docType, docNo, docSrNo) {
  return normalizeAllocationCode(docType) + '|' + normalizeAllocationCode(docNo) + '|' + (parseInt(docSrNo, 10) || 0);
}

// Get COA meta (subledger flag + subledger type) for an account code
function getAllocationAccountMeta(ss, acCode) {
  var meta = { found: false, subledgerExists: 0, actypeDocno: '', description: '' };
  if (!acCode) return meta;
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  if (!coaSheet) return meta;
  var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var wantedCode = normalizeAllocationCode(acCode);
  for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
    if (coaData[i][0] && normalizeAllocationCode(coaData[i][0]) === wantedCode) {
      meta.found = true;
      meta.subledgerExists = parseInt(coaData[i][8], 10) || 0;
      meta.actypeDocno = normalizeAllocationCode(coaData[i][13]);
      meta.description = coaData[i][2] || '';
      return meta;
    }
  }
  return meta;
}

// Allocation policy for an account: allowed + account/party/To-document types
function getAllocationPolicy(ss, acCode) {
  var meta = getAllocationAccountMeta(ss, acCode);
  var policy = { allowed: false, hasSubledger: false, accountType: '', subledgerType: '', toDocType: '', targetTypes: [], strictParty: true, description: meta.description };
  policy.hasSubledger = meta.subledgerExists === 1;
