  if (policy.hasSubledger && meta.actypeDocno === 'AR') {
    policy.allowed = true;
    policy.accountType = 'AR';
    policy.subledgerType = 'Customer';
    policy.toDocType = 'SI';
  } else if (policy.hasSubledger && meta.actypeDocno === 'AP') {
    policy.allowed = true;
    policy.accountType = 'AP';
    policy.subledgerType = 'Supplier';
    policy.toDocType = 'PI';
  }
  if (policy.allowed) {
    // The invoices of the account are always valid targets (SI for AR, PI for AP).
    // Other ledger lines of the same account + subledger (unapplied receipts /
    // payments, journal lines) can be enabled with the system parameter
    // AllocationAllowVoucherDocs = 1.
    policy.targetTypes = [policy.toDocType];
    var allowVoucherDocsValue = String(getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'AllocationAllowVoucherDocs', '')).replace(/\s+/g, '').toUpperCase();
    if (['1', 'Y', 'YES', 'TRUE', 'ON'].indexOf(allowVoucherDocsValue) !== -1) {
      policy.targetTypes = policy.targetTypes.concat(['CRV', 'BRV', 'CPV', 'BPV', 'JV']);
    }
    // Same party rule: strict by default (a receipt can only settle documents of
    // its own party). AllocationStrictParty = 0 allows any party of the account.
    var strictPartyValue = String(getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'AllocationStrictParty', '')).replace(/\s+/g, '').toUpperCase();
    policy.strictParty = ['0', 'N', 'NO', 'FALSE', 'OFF'].indexOf(strictPartyValue) === -1;
  }
  return policy;
}

// Is this document type a valid allocation target for the account?
function isAllowedTargetType(policy, docType) {
  if (!policy || !policy.allowed) return false;
  var types = (policy.targetTypes && policy.targetTypes.length) ? policy.targetTypes : [policy.toDocType];
  var wantedType = normalizeAllocationCode(docType);
  if (!wantedType) return false;
  for (var i = 0; i < types.length; i++) {
    if (normalizeAllocationCode(types[i]) === wantedType) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// Get the pending (unallocated) documents of a party: AccountTransaction rows
// of the AR/AP control account + subledger that still have a BAL_AMOUNT.
// addBackMap holds allocations that are not posted yet (this voucher's own
// pending allocations) so they can be re-allocated while editing.
function getOutstandingDocsForParty(ss, acCode, subledgerDocNo, policy, addBackMap, fromAmount, excludeDocType, excludeDocNo) {
  var docs = [];
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (!accountTransactionSheet) return docs;
  var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var wantedAccount = normalizeAllocationCode(acCode);
  var wantedSubledger = normalizeAllocationCode(subledgerDocNo);
  var wantedFromAmount = parseFloat(fromAmount) || 0;
  var wantedExcludeType = normalizeAllocationCode(excludeDocType);
  var wantedExcludeDocNo = normalizeAllocationCode(excludeDocNo);
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var docType = normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][0]);
    if (!isAllowedTargetType(policy, docType)) continue;
    // The voucher itself can never be its own target
    if (wantedExcludeType && docType === wantedExcludeType && normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][1]) === wantedExcludeDocNo) continue;
    if (normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][3]) !== wantedAccount) continue;
    if (wantedSubledger && normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][4]) !== wantedSubledger) continue;
    var docNo = String(data[i][1] || '');
    var docSrNo = parseInt(data[i][2], 10) || 0;
    var balAmount = Math.round((parseFloat(data[i][9]) || 0) * 100) / 100;
    // Only a document with the opposite balance direction can be settled
    // (a receipt settles a debit line, a payment settles a credit line)
    if (Math.abs(wantedFromAmount) > 0.005 && balAmount * wantedFromAmount >= 0) continue;
    var outstanding = Math.round(Math.abs(balAmount) * 100) / 100;
    var addBack = Math.round((((addBackMap || {})[allocationDocKey(docType, docNo, docSrNo)]) || 0) * 100) / 100;
    var available = Math.round((outstanding + addBack) * 100) / 100;
    if (available <= 0) continue;
    docs.push({
      toDocType: docType,
      toDocNo: docNo,
      toDocSrNo: docSrNo,
      subledgerDocNo: String(data[i][4] || ''),
      date: data[i][6] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][6]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
      narration: data[i][7] || '',
      refNo: data[i][10] || '',
      docAmount: Math.round(Math.abs(parseFloat(data[i][8]) || 0) * 100) / 100,
      balance: balAmount,
      outstanding: outstanding,
      allocated: addBack,
      available: available
    });
  }
  docs.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return a.date < b.date ? -1 : 1;
    if (a.toDocNo !== b.toDocNo) return a.toDocNo < b.toDocNo ? -1 : 1;
    return a.toDocSrNo - b.toDocSrNo;
  });
  return docs;
}

// Read the pending allocation drafts of a voucher from
// JournalVoucherDetails.AllocationJSON (returns [{docSrNo, acCode,
// subledger, amount, narration, allocations[]}])
function getAllocationDraftsForVoucher(ss, docType, docNo) {
  var drafts = [];
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) return drafts;
  var data = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var wantedType = normalizeAllocationCode(docType);
  var wantedDocNo = normalizeAllocationCode(docNo);
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][0]) !== wantedType) continue;
    if (normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][1]) !== wantedDocNo) continue;
    var allocations = [];
    var rawJson = data[i][ALLOCATION_COLUMN - 1];
    if (rawJson) {
      try {
        allocations = JSON.parse(rawJson) || [];
      } catch (err) {
        allocations = [];
      }
    }
    drafts.push({
      docSrNo: parseInt(data[i][2], 10) || 0,
      acCode: data[i][3] || '',
      subledger: data[i][5] || '',
      amount: parseFloat(data[i][6]) || 0,
      narration: data[i][7] || '',
      allocations: allocations
    });
  }
  return drafts;
}

// Store the pending allocations of a voucher into JournalVoucherDetails.AllocationJSON
function saveAllocationDraftsToSheet(ss, docType, docNo, details) {
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) return;
  ensureDetailAllocationColumn(ss, detailsSheet);
  var data = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var wantedType = normalizeAllocationCode(docType);
  var wantedDocNo = normalizeAllocationCode(docNo);
  for (var d = 0; d < (details || []).length; d++) {
    var detail = details[d];
    var docSrNo = parseInt(detail.docSrNo, 10) || (d + 1);
    var allocations = detail.allocations || [];
    var json = allocations.length > 0 ? JSON.stringify(allocations) : '';
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][0]) !== wantedType) continue;
      if (normalizeAllocationCode(data[i][1]) !== wantedDocNo) continue;
      if ((parseInt(data[i][2], 10) || 0) !== docSrNo) continue;
      detailsSheet.getRange(i + 1, ALLOCATION_COLUMN).setValue(json);
      break;
    }
  }
}

// Validate the pending allocations of a voucher (details[] entries must have
// docSrNo, acCode, subledger, amount and allocations[]).
// Rules: the From account must have a subledger of type AR/AP, the To document
// must exist in the ledger for the same account + subledger, and neither the
// line amount nor the invoice balance may be exceeded.
function validateAllocationSet(ss, details) {
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  var atData = accountTransactionSheet ? accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var policyCache = {};
  var toLineTotals = {}; // toKey -> { total, balance, docNo }
  for (var d = 0; d < (details || []).length; d++) {
    var detail = details[d];
    var allocations = detail.allocations || [];
    if (allocations.length === 0) continue;
    var acCode = normalizeAllocationCode(detail.acCode);
    if (!policyCache[acCode]) policyCache[acCode] = getAllocationPolicy(ss, acCode);
    var policy = policyCache[acCode];
    if (!policy.allowed) {
      return { valid: false, message: 'Account ' + acCode + ' cannot be allocated. Allocation is allowed only for accounts that have a subledger of type AR or AP.' };
    }
    var subledger = String(detail.subledger || '');
    if (!subledger) {
      return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': select a ' + policy.subledgerType + ' before allocating.' };
    }
    var lineAmount = Math.round(Math.abs(parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0) * 100) / 100;
    var lineTotal = 0;
    for (var a = 0; a < allocations.length; a++) {
      var alloc = allocations[a];
      var allocAmount = Math.round(Math.abs(parseFloat(alloc.amount) || 0) * 100) / 100;
      if (!alloc.toDocType || !alloc.toDocNo) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': allocation is missing the document details.' };
      }
      if (allocAmount <= 0) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': allocation amount must be greater than zero.' };
      }
      var toDocType = normalizeAllocationCode(alloc.toDocType);
      if (!isAllowedTargetType(policy, toDocType)) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': ' + toDocType + ' documents cannot be allocated for account ' + acCode + '.' };
      }
      var toDocNo = String(alloc.toDocNo);
      var toDocSrNo = parseInt(alloc.toDocSrNo, 10) || 0;
      var toKey = allocationDocKey(toDocType, toDocNo, toDocSrNo);
      var atRow = -1;
      for (var i = 1; i < atData.length; i++) {
        if (normalizeAllocationCode(atData[i][0]) !== toDocType) continue;
        if (normalizeAllocationCode(atData[i][1]) !== normalizeAllocationCode(toDocNo)) continue;
        if ((parseInt(atData[i][2], 10) || 0) !== toDocSrNo) continue;
        atRow = i;
        break;
      }
      if (atRow === -1) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': ' + toDocNo + ' line ' + toDocSrNo + ' was not found in the ledger.' };
      }
      if (normalizeAllocationCode(atData[atRow][3]) !== acCode) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': ' + toDocNo + ' does not belong to account ' + acCode + '.' };
      }
      if (policy.strictParty && normalizeAllocationCode(atData[atRow][4]) !== normalizeAllocationCode(subledger)) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': ' + toDocNo + ' does not belong to ' + subledger + '.' };
      }
      var balAmount = Math.round((parseFloat(atData[atRow][9]) || 0) * 100) / 100;
      if (Math.abs(balAmount) < 0.005) {
        return { valid: false, message: toDocNo + ' is already fully allocated.' };
      }
      if (balAmount * (parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0) >= 0) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': ' + toDocNo + ' has the same balance direction as this line and cannot be settled by it.' };
      }
      if (!toLineTotals[toKey]) {
        toLineTotals[toKey] = { total: 0, balance: Math.round(Math.abs(balAmount) * 100) / 100, docNo: toDocNo };
      }
      toLineTotals[toKey].total = Math.round((toLineTotals[toKey].total + allocAmount) * 100) / 100;
      if (toLineTotals[toKey].total > toLineTotals[toKey].balance + 0.01) {
        return { valid: false, message: 'Allocation to ' + toDocNo + ' exceeds its outstanding balance (' + toLineTotals[toKey].balance.toFixed(2) + ').' };
      }
      lineTotal = Math.round((lineTotal + allocAmount) * 100) / 100;
    }
    if (lineTotal > lineAmount + 0.01) {
      return { valid: false, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': total allocation ' + lineTotal.toFixed(2) + ' exceeds the line amount ' + lineAmount.toFixed(2) + '.' };
    }
  }
  return { valid: true, message: '' };
}

// Write the pending allocations of a posted voucher:
//  - one row per allocation in the Allocation sheet
//  - From line BAL_AMOUNT += Amount, To line BAL_AMOUNT -= Amount
// Returns { success, rows, amount, message }
function applyVoucherAllocations(ss, docType, docNo) {
  var drafts = getAllocationDraftsForVoucher(ss, docType, docNo);
  var list = [];
  for (var d = 0; d < drafts.length; d++) {
    var detail = drafts[d];
    var allocations = detail.allocations || [];
    if (allocations.length === 0) continue;
    var policy = getAllocationPolicy(ss, detail.acCode);
    if (!policy.allowed) {
      return { success: false, rows: 0, amount: 0, message: 'Account ' + detail.acCode + ' cannot be allocated.' };
    }
    if (!String(detail.subledger || '')) {
      return { success: false, rows: 0, amount: 0, message: 'Line ' + detail.docSrNo + ': a subledger is required for allocation.' };
    }
    // The allocation amount carries the opposite sign of the From line amount
    // (receipt vouchers allocate negative amounts, payment vouchers positive)
    var lineSign = (parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0) < 0 ? 1 : -1;
    for (var a = 0; a < allocations.length; a++) {
      var alloc = allocations[a];
      var allocAmount = Math.round(Math.abs(parseFloat(alloc.amount) || 0) * 100) / 100;
      if (allocAmount <= 0) continue;
      list.push({
        fromDocSrNo: parseInt(detail.docSrNo, 10) || 0,
        accountType: policy.accountType,
        subledgerDocNo: detail.subledger,
        toDocType: String(alloc.toDocType).toUpperCase(),
        toDocNo: String(alloc.toDocNo),
        toDocSrNo: parseInt(alloc.toDocSrNo, 10) || 0,
        amount: Math.round(lineSign * allocAmount * 100) / 100
      });
    }
  }
  if (list.length === 0) {
    return { success: true, rows: 0, amount: 0, message: '' };
  }
  
  var allocationSheet = ensureAllocationSheet(ss);
  var fromSrSrCounters = {};
  var toSrSrCounters = {};
  var existing = allocationSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < existing.length; i++) {
    if (!existing[i][0]) continue;
    var seedFromKey = allocationDocKey(existing[i][0], existing[i][1], existing[i][2]);
    var seedFromSrSr = parseInt(existing[i][3], 10) || 0;
    if (!fromSrSrCounters[seedFromKey] || seedFromSrSr > fromSrSrCounters[seedFromKey]) fromSrSrCounters[seedFromKey] = seedFromSrSr;
    var seedToKey = allocationDocKey(existing[i][6], existing[i][7], existing[i][8]);
    var seedToSrSr = parseInt(existing[i][9], 10) || 0;
    if (!toSrSrCounters[seedToKey] || seedToSrSr > toSrSrCounters[seedToKey]) toSrSrCounters[seedToKey] = seedToSrSr;
  }
  
  var rows = [];
  var fromDeltas = {};
  var toDeltas = {};
  var totalAmount = 0;
  for (var n = 0; n < list.length; n++) {
    var item = list[n];
    var fromKey = allocationDocKey(docType, docNo, item.fromDocSrNo);
    var toKey = allocationDocKey(item.toDocType, item.toDocNo, item.toDocSrNo);
    fromSrSrCounters[fromKey] = (fromSrSrCounters[fromKey] || 0) + 1;
    toSrSrCounters[toKey] = (toSrSrCounters[toKey] || 0) + 1;
    rows.push([
      docType, docNo, item.fromDocSrNo, fromSrSrCounters[fromKey],
      item.accountType, item.subledgerDocNo,
      item.toDocType, item.toDocNo, item.toDocSrNo, toSrSrCounters[toKey],
      item.amount
    ]);
    fromDeltas[fromKey] = Math.round(((fromDeltas[fromKey] || 0) + item.amount) * 100) / 100;
    toDeltas[toKey] = Math.round(((toDeltas[toKey] || 0) + item.amount) * 100) / 100;
    totalAmount = Math.round((totalAmount + Math.abs(item.amount)) * 100) / 100;
  }
  
  // Append the allocation rows
  for (var r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
    allocationSheet.appendRow(rows[r]);
  }
  
  // Update the BAL_AMOUNT of both sides in AccountTransaction
  var accountTransactionSheet = ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss);
  var atData = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var atKeyIndex = {};
  for (var k = 1; k < atData.length; k++) {
    if (!atData[k][0]) continue;
    var atKey = allocationDocKey(atData[k][0], atData[k][1], atData[k][2]);
    if (atKeyIndex[atKey] === undefined) atKeyIndex[atKey] = k;
  }
  var balanceChanged = false;
  for (var f in fromDeltas) {
    var fromIndex = atKeyIndex[f];
    if (fromIndex === undefined) continue;
    atData[fromIndex][9] = Math.round(((parseFloat(atData[fromIndex][9]) || 0) + fromDeltas[f]) * 100) / 100;
    balanceChanged = true;
  }
  for (var t in toDeltas) {
    var toIndex = atKeyIndex[t];
    if (toIndex === undefined) continue;
    atData[toIndex][9] = Math.round(((parseFloat(atData[toIndex][9]) || 0) - toDeltas[t]) * 100) / 100;
    balanceChanged = true;
  }
  if (balanceChanged && atData.length > 1) {
    var balanceValues = [];
    for (var b = 1; b < atData.length; b++) {
      balanceValues.push([atData[b][9]]);
    }
    accountTransactionSheet.getRange(2, 10, balanceValues.length, 1).setValues(balanceValues);
  }
  
  return { success: true, rows: rows.length, amount: totalAmount, message: '' };
}

// Remove the allocation rows of a voucher and restore the invoice balances.
// Called when the post of the voucher is cancelled.
function reverseVoucherAllocations(ss, docType, docNo) {
  var allocationSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Allocation");
  if (!allocationSheet) return { success: true, rows: 0 };
  var data = allocationSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var toDeltas = {};
  var rowsToDelete = [];
  var wantedType = String(docType || '').toUpperCase();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0] || '').toUpperCase() !== wantedType) continue;
    if (String(data[i][1] || '') !== String(docNo)) continue;
    var amount = Math.round((parseFloat(data[i][10]) || 0) * 100) / 100;
    var toKey = allocationDocKey(data[i][6], data[i][7], data[i][8]);
    toDeltas[toKey] = Math.round(((toDeltas[toKey] || 0) + amount) * 100) / 100;
    rowsToDelete.push(i + 1);
  }
  if (rowsToDelete.length === 0) return { success: true, rows: 0 };
  
  for (var r = rowsToDelete.length - 1; r >= 0; r--) {
    allocationSheet.deleteRow(rowsToDelete[r]);
  }
  
  // The invoice balance was reduced by the allocation amount, so add it back
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var atData = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var changed = false;
    for (var k = 1; k < atData.length; k++) {
      if (!atData[k][0]) continue;
      var atKey = allocationDocKey(atData[k][0], atData[k][1], atData[k][2]);
      if (toDeltas[atKey] === undefined) continue;
      atData[k][9] = Math.round(((parseFloat(atData[k][9]) || 0) + toDeltas[atKey]) * 100) / 100;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (changed && atData.length > 1) {
      var balanceValues = [];
      for (var b = 1; b < atData.length; b++) {
        balanceValues.push([atData[b][9]]);
      }
      accountTransactionSheet.getRange(2, 10, balanceValues.length, 1).setValues(balanceValues);
    }
  }
  
  return { success: true, rows: rowsToDelete.length };
}

// Shared error response for the allocation handlers
function allocationErrorResponse(callback, message) {
  var errorResult = { status: 'error', message: message };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
}

// Get the pending documents of an account + subledger (SI for AR, PI for AP,
// plus the voucher documents when AllocationAllowVoucherDocs = 1).
// fromAmount is the signed amount of the voucher line (used to keep only the
// documents with the opposite balance direction), excludeDocType/excludeDocNo
// keep the voucher itself out of its own target list.
// currentAllocations (optional JSON) holds the allocations the form has not
// posted yet, so they can be re-allocated while editing.
function handleGetAllocationOutstandingGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var acCode = e.parameter.acCode || '';
  var subledgerDocNo = e.parameter.subledgerDocNo || '';
  var docType = e.parameter.docType || '';
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo || '';
  var fromAmount = e.parameter.fromAmount || '';
  var currentAllocationsJson = e.parameter.currentAllocations || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  // The voucher itself can never be one of its own allocation targets
  var excludeDocType = e.parameter.excludeDocType || docType;
  var excludeDocNo = e.parameter.excludeDocNo || docNo;
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    return allocationErrorResponse(callback, 'Invalid or expired session');
  }
  if (!acCode) {
    return allocationErrorResponse(callback, 'Account code is required');
  }
  
  var policy = getAllocationPolicy(ss, acCode);
  if (!policy.allowed) {
    return allocationErrorResponse(callback, 'Allocation is allowed only for accounts that have a subledger of type AR or AP');
  }
  if (!subledgerDocNo) {
    return allocationErrorResponse(callback, 'Select a ' + policy.subledgerType + ' before allocating');
  }
  
  // Collect the allocations that are not posted yet
  var addBackMap = {};
  var addBackFromClient = false;
  if (currentAllocationsJson) {
    try {
      var clientAllocations = JSON.parse(currentAllocationsJson) || [];
      addBackFromClient = true;
      for (var c = 0; c < clientAllocations.length; c++) {
        var clientKey = allocationDocKey(clientAllocations[c].toDocType, clientAllocations[c].toDocNo, clientAllocations[c].toDocSrNo);
        addBackMap[clientKey] = Math.round(((addBackMap[clientKey] || 0) + Math.abs(parseFloat(clientAllocations[c].amount) || 0)) * 100) / 100;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      addBackFromClient = false;
      addBackMap = {};
    }
  }
  if (!addBackFromClient && docType && docNo) {
    var drafts = getAllocationDraftsForVoucher(ss, docType, docNo);
    for (var d = 0; d < drafts.length; d++) {
      var draftAllocations = drafts[d].allocations || [];
      for (var a = 0; a < draftAllocations.length; a++) {
        var draftKey = allocationDocKey(draftAllocations[a].toDocType, draftAllocations[a].toDocNo, draftAllocations[a].toDocSrNo);
        addBackMap[draftKey] = Math.round(((addBackMap[draftKey] || 0) + Math.abs(parseFloat(draftAllocations[a].amount) || 0)) * 100) / 100;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Party filter: strict by default, any party of the account when
  // AllocationStrictParty = 0
  var wantedSubledger = policy.strictParty ? subledgerDocNo : '';
  var documents = getOutstandingDocsForParty(ss, acCode, wantedSubledger, policy, addBackMap, fromAmount, excludeDocType, excludeDocNo);
  
  // When nothing is found for this party, also look for the same account +
  // direction of any party so the form can explain the empty list
  var similarDocuments = [];
  if (documents.length === 0 && wantedSubledger) {
    var otherDocuments = getOutstandingDocsForParty(ss, acCode, '', policy, addBackMap, fromAmount, excludeDocType, excludeDocNo);
    for (var s = 0; s < otherDocuments.length && similarDocuments.length < 5; s++) {
      similarDocuments.push(otherDocuments[s]);
    }
  }
  
  var result = {
    status: 'success',
    accountType: policy.accountType,
    accountDescription: policy.description,
    subledgerType: policy.subledgerType,
    subledgerDocNo: subledgerDocNo,
    toDocType: policy.toDocType,
    targetTypes: policy.targetTypes,
    allowVoucherDocs: policy.targetTypes.length > 1,
    strictParty: policy.strictParty,
    documents: documents,
    similarDocuments: similarDocuments
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get the saved (pending) allocations of a voucher, grouped by detail serial number
function handleGetVoucherAllocationsGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docType = e.parameter.docType || '';
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    return allocationErrorResponse(callback, 'Invalid or expired session');
  }
  if (!docType || !docNo) {
    return allocationErrorResponse(callback, 'DocType and DocNo are required');
  }
  
  var drafts = getAllocationDraftsForVoucher(ss, docType, docNo);
  var allocationsBySrNo = {};
  var total = 0;
  var count = 0;
  for (var d = 0; d < drafts.length; d++) {
    var allocations = drafts[d].allocations || [];
    var normalized = [];
    for (var a = 0; a < allocations.length; a++) {
      var amount = Math.round(Math.abs(parseFloat(allocations[a].amount) || 0) * 100) / 100;
      if (amount <= 0) continue;
      normalized.push({
        toDocType: String(allocations[a].toDocType || '').toUpperCase(),
        toDocNo: String(allocations[a].toDocNo || ''),
        toDocSrNo: parseInt(allocations[a].toDocSrNo, 10) || 0,
        amount: amount
      });
      total = Math.round((total + amount) * 100) / 100;
      count++;
    }
    if (normalized.length > 0) {
      allocationsBySrNo[String(drafts[d].docSrNo)] = normalized;
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: 'success', docType: docType, docNo: docNo, allocationsBySrNo: allocationsBySrNo, count: count, total: total };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// ALLOCATION DIAGNOSTIC (read only)
// ============================================
// Run this from the Apps Script editor (select ALLOC_DIAGNOSTIC and click Run)
// to log every ledger line the allocation engine can use, exactly as the picker
// reads it. Nothing is modified.
function ALLOC_DIAGNOSTIC() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var L = [];
  var CAP = 300;
  
  L.push('=== 1) COA rows with SUBLEDGER_EXISTS = 1  (AC_CODE | AC_TYPE | ACTYPE_DOCNO | DESCRIPTION) ===');
  var coa = ss.getSheetByName('COA');
  if (coa) {
    var c = coa.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < c.length; i++) {
      if (parseInt(c[i][8], 10) === 1) {
        L.push('  ' + [String(c[i][0] || ''), String(c[i][1] || ''), String(c[i][13] || ''), String(c[i][2] || '')].join(' | '));
      }
    }
  } else {
    L.push('  (no COA sheet)');
  }
  
  var at = ss.getSheetByName('AccountTransaction');
  L.push('');
  L.push('=== 2a) Outstanding party lines grouped: DOCTYPE | AC_DOCNO | SUBLEDGER -> lines | total ===');
  L.push('=== 2b) The same keys, marked as target / not a target ===');
  L.push('=== 2c) Raw transactional rows (DOCTYPE | DOCNO | SR | AC_DOCNO | SUBLEDGER | AMOUNT | BAL_AMOUNT | REF_NO) ===');
  if (at) {
    var a = at.getDataRange().getValues();
    var group = {};
    var groupKeys = [];
    var raw = [];
    for (var j = 1; j < a.length; j++) {
      var dt = normalizeAllocationCode(a[j][0]);
      var ac = normalizeAllocationCode(a[j][3]);
      var sub = normalizeAllocationCode(a[j][4]);
      var amt = parseFloat(a[j][8]) || 0;
      var bal = Math.round((parseFloat(a[j][9]) || 0) * 100) / 100;
      var isTxnDoc = (dt === 'SI' || dt === 'PI' || dt === 'CRV' || dt === 'BRV' || dt === 'CPV' || dt === 'BPV' || dt === 'JV');
      if ((isTxnDoc || sub !== '') && raw.length < CAP) {
        raw.push([dt, String(a[j][1] || ''), a[j][2], ac, sub, amt, bal, String(a[j][10] || '')].join(' | '));
      }
      if (sub !== '' && Math.abs(bal) > 0.005) {
        var key = dt + ' | ' + ac + ' | ' + sub;
        if (!group[key]) {
          group[key] = { n: 0, t: 0 };
          groupKeys.push(key);
        }
        group[key].n++;
        group[key].t = Math.round((group[key].t + Math.abs(bal)) * 100) / 100;
      }
    }
    for (var g = 0; g < groupKeys.length; g++) {
      var k1 = groupKeys[g];
      L.push('  ' + k1 + '  -> lines=' + group[k1].n + '  total=' + group[k1].t);
    }
    L.push('--- 2b (AR targets = SI, AP targets = PI; voucher types only when AllocationAllowVoucherDocs = 1) ---');
    for (var g2 = 0; g2 < groupKeys.length; g2++) {
      var k2 = groupKeys[g2];
      var first = k2.split(' | ')[0];
      var isTarget = (first === 'SI' || first === 'PI');
      L.push('  ' + k2 + (isTarget ? '   <-- INVOICE: valid target' : '   <-- voucher/other: target only with AllocationAllowVoucherDocs = 1'));
    }
    L.push('--- 2c (first ' + CAP + ' rows) ---');
    for (var r = 0; r < raw.length; r++) L.push('  ' + raw[r]);
  } else {
    L.push('  (no AccountTransaction sheet)');
  }
  
  L.push('');
  L.push('=== 3) Voucher lines (TYPE | DOCNO | SR | AC | SUBLEDGER | AMOUNT | col M) ===');
  var jvd = ss.getSheetByName('JournalVoucherDetails');
  if (jvd) {
    var d = jvd.getDataRange().getValues();
    L.push('  header M1 = [' + String(d[0][12] || '(empty)') + ']   (total columns: ' + d[0].length + ')');
    var n3 = 0;
    for (var k = 1; k < d.length; k++) {
      var sub3 = normalizeAllocationCode(d[k][5]);
      if (sub3 !== '' || String(d[k][12] || '') !== '') {
        L.push('  ' + [String(d[k][0] || ''), String(d[k][1] || ''), String(d[k][2] || ''), String(d[k][3] || ''), sub3, (parseFloat(d[k][6]) || 0), String(d[k][12] || '')].join(' | '));
        n3++;
      }
      if (n3 > CAP) {
        L.push('  ... (truncated)');
        break;
      }
    }
  } else {
    L.push('  (no JournalVoucherDetails sheet)');
  }
  
  L.push('');
  L.push('=== 4) Allocation sheet ===');
  var al = ss.getSheetByName('Allocation');
  if (al) {
    var r2 = al.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var m = 0; m < r2.length && m <= 100; m++) L.push('  ' + r2[m].join(' | '));
    L.push('  rows = ' + Math.max(0, r2.length - 1));
  } else {
    L.push('  (no Allocation sheet yet)');
  }
  
  Logger.log(L.join('\n'));
}

// ============================================
// STATEMENT OF ACCOUNT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get ledger/statement of account (by GL account, optionally filtered by party for AR/AP)
function handleGetStatementOfAccountGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var acCode = e.parameter.acCode || '';
  var partyType = e.parameter.partyType || '';
  var partyCode = e.parameter.partyCode || '';
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!acCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account code is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Look up account description from COA
  var accountDescription = acCode;
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  var coaDescMap = {};
  if (coaSheet) {
    var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
      var acType = String(coaData[i][1] || '');
      var accountCode = String(coaData[i][0] || '');
      var desc = coaData[i][2] || '';
      if (accountCode) {
        coaDescMap[accountCode] = desc;
        if (accountCode === acCode) {
          accountDescription = desc;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Look up party name from CustomerMaster or SupplierMaster (if partyCode provided)
  var partyName = partyCode;
  if (partyCode) {
    if (partyType === 'Customer') {
      var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
      if (customerSheet) {
        var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (var c = 1; c < customerData.length; c++) {
          if (customerData[c][0] && String(customerData[c][0]).toUpperCase() == String(partyCode).toUpperCase()) {
            partyName = customerData[c][1] || partyCode;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    } else {
      var supplierSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SupplierMaster");
      if (supplierSheet) {
        var supplierData = supplierSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (var s = 1; s < supplierData.length; s++) {
          if (supplierData[s][0] && String(supplierData[s][0]).toUpperCase() == String(partyCode).toUpperCase()) {
            partyName = supplierData[s][1] || partyCode;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (!accountTransactionSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { 
      status: "success", 
      account: { code: acCode, description: accountDescription },
      party: partyCode ? { type: partyType, code: partyCode, name: partyName } : null,
      entries: [], 
      summary: { openingBalance: 0, totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0, closingBalance: 0 } 
    };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allEntries = [];
  var openingBalance = 0;
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  var totalOutstanding = 0;
  var pendingBills = 0;
  
  // Collect all entries for this account (and party if provided) across all dates first
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var accountCode = String(data[i][3] || '');
    if (accountCode === acCode) {
      // If partyCode provided, filter by subledger too
      if (partyCode) {
        var subledger = String(data[i][4] || '');
        if (normalizeAllocationCode(subledger) !== normalizeAllocationCode(partyCode)) {
          continue;
        }
      }
      
      var valDate = data[i][6] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][6]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      var amount = parseFloat(data[i][8]) || 0;
      var docType = String(data[i][0] || '');
      var partySubledger = String(data[i][4] || '');
      
      // If before fromDate, add to opening balance
      if (fromDate && valDate < fromDate) {
        openingBalance += amount;
        continue;
      }
      
      // Skip entries after toDate
      if (toDate && valDate > toDate) {
        continue;
      }
      
      // Add to totals
      if (amount < 0) {
        totalDebit += Math.abs(amount);
      } else {
        totalCredit += amount;
      }
      
      // Pending amount of this document (allocation keeps BAL_AMOUNT up to date)
      var balAmount = Math.round((parseFloat(data[i][9]) || 0) * 100) / 100;
      if (partySubledger && Math.abs(balAmount) > 0.01) {
        totalOutstanding = Math.round((totalOutstanding + Math.abs(balAmount)) * 100) / 100;
        pendingBills++;
      }
      
      allEntries.push({
        docType: docType,
        docNo: String(data[i][1] || ''),
        acDocNo: accountCode,
        accountDescription: coaDescMap[accountCode] || '',
        subledger: partySubledger,
        date: valDate,
        narration: data[i][7] || '',
        amount: amount,
        debit: amount < 0 ? Math.abs(amount) : 0,
        credit: amount > 0 ? amount : 0,
        balanceAmount: balAmount,
        outstanding: partySubledger ? Math.abs(balAmount) : 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date ascending, then by docNo ascending
  allEntries.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);
    return a.docNo.localeCompare(b.docNo);
  });
  
  // Calculate running balance for each entry
  var runningBalance = openingBalance;
  for (var j = 0; j < allEntries.length; j++) {
    runningBalance += allEntries[j].amount;
    allEntries[j].runningBalance = Math.round(runningBalance * 100) / 100;
  }
  
  var closingBalance = runningBalance;
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    account: { code: acCode, description: accountDescription },
    party: partyCode ? { type: partyType, code: partyCode, name: partyName } : null,
    entries: allEntries,
    summary: {
      openingBalance: Math.round(openingBalance * 100) / 100,
      totalDebit: Math.round(totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(totalCredit * 100) / 100,
      closingBalance: Math.round(closingBalance * 100) / 100,
      totalOutstanding: Math.round(totalOutstanding * 100) / 100,
      pendingBills: pendingBills
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// TRIAL BALANCE HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get trial balance (GL accounts with total debits and credits for a date range)
function handleGetTrialBalanceGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read the full COA hierarchy (groups + GL ledgers)
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  var coaByDocno = {}; // docno -> account record
  var coaMap = {};     // acCode -> account record
  var allGlCodes = {}; // acCode -> account record (only GL / posting accounts)
  if (coaSheet) {
    var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
      var acCode = String(coaData[i][0] || '');
      var acType = String(coaData[i][1] || '');
      var desc = coaData[i][2] || '';
      var level = parseInt(coaData[i][3]) || 1;
      var parent = parseInt(coaData[i][4]) || 0;
      var docno = parseInt(coaData[i][5]) || 0;
      if (!acCode && !docno) continue;
      
      var rec = { acCode: acCode, acType: acType, description: desc, level: level, parent: parent, docno: docno };
      if (docno) coaByDocno[docno] = rec;
      if (acCode) {
        coaMap[acCode] = rec;
        if (acType === 'GL') {
          allGlCodes[acCode] = rec;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  var totalsMap = {}; // acCode -> { totalDebit, totalCredit }
  
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var acDocNo = String(data[i][3] || '');
      if (!acDocNo) continue;
      
      // Apply date range filter on VALDATE (index 6)
      var valDate = '';
      if (data[i][6]) {
        try {
          valDate = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][6]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
        } catch (e) {
          valDate = '';
        }
      }
      if (fromDate && valDate < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && valDate > toDate) continue;
      
      var amount = parseFloat(data[i][8]) || 0;
      
      // Only include accounts that exist in COA as GL accounts
      var coaInfo = coaMap[acDocNo];
      if (!coaInfo || coaInfo.acType !== 'GL') continue;
      
      if (!totalsMap[acDocNo]) {
        totalsMap[acDocNo] = { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
      }
      
      if (amount < 0) {
        totalsMap[acDocNo].totalDebit += Math.abs(amount);
      } else {
        totalsMap[acDocNo].totalCredit += amount;
      }
    }
  }  
  // All GL accounts to show: every GL account in COA (including zero balance) plus any with transactions
  var allCodes = {};
  for (var code in allGlCodes) {
    if (allGlCodes.hasOwnProperty(code)) {
      allCodes[code] = true;
    }
  }
  for (var code2 in totalsMap) {
    if (totalsMap.hasOwnProperty(code2)) {
      allCodes[code2] = true;
    }
  }
  
  // Compute per-ledger figures
  var ledgerInfo = {}; // acCode -> ledger figures + hierarchy info
  var totalAccounts = 0;
  for (var ac in allCodes) {
    if (!allCodes.hasOwnProperty(ac)) continue;
    var info = coaMap[ac];
    if (!info) continue;
    var t = totalsMap[ac] || { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
    var netDebit = 0;
    var netCredit = 0;
    var net = t.totalDebit - t.totalCredit;
    if (net > 0) {
      netDebit = net;
    } else {
      netCredit = Math.abs(net);
    }
    ledgerInfo[ac] = {
      acCode: ac,
      description: info.description,
      level: info.level,
      parent: info.parent,
      docno: info.docno,
      totalDebit: Math.round(t.totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(t.totalCredit * 100) / 100,
      netDebit: Math.round(netDebit * 100) / 100,
      netCredit: Math.round(netCredit * 100) / 100
    };
    totalAccounts++;
  }
  
  // Build parent -> children map from the full COA hierarchy
  var childrenMap = {};
  for (var d in coaByDocno) {
    if (coaByDocno.hasOwnProperty(d)) {
      var rec = coaByDocno[d];
      var p = rec.parent || 0;
      if (!childrenMap[p]) childrenMap[p] = [];
      childrenMap[p].push(rec);
    }
  }
  for (var cm in childrenMap) {
    if (childrenMap.hasOwnProperty(cm)) {
      childrenMap[cm].sort(function(a, b) { return a.docno - b.docno; });
    }
  }  
  // Walk the COA tree and emit rows in tree order.
  // Group rows are headers only (no amounts); only groups that lead to at
  // least one GL ledger are included.
  var rows = [];
  var includedLedgers = {}; // acCode -> true
  
  function pushLedgerRow(rec, depth) {
    var lr = ledgerInfo[rec.acCode];
    if (!lr) return false;
    rows.push({
      type: 'ledger',
      level: depth,
      docno: rec.docno,
      parent: rec.parent,
      acCode: rec.acCode,
      description: rec.description,
      totalDebit: lr.totalDebit,
      totalCredit: lr.totalCredit,
      netDebit: lr.netDebit,
      netCredit: lr.netCredit,
      hasChildren: false
    });
    includedLedgers[rec.acCode] = true;
    return true;
  }
  
  function buildNode(node, depth) {
    var kids = childrenMap[node.docno] || [];
    var startIdx = rows.length;
    var hasLedger = false;
    for (var k = 0; k < kids.length; k++) {
      var child = kids[k];
      if (child.acType === 'GL' && ledgerInfo[child.acCode]) {
        if (pushLedgerRow(child, depth + 1)) hasLedger = true;
      } else {
        if (buildNode(child, depth + 1)) hasLedger = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasLedger && node.acType !== 'GL') {
      rows.splice(startIdx, 0, {
        type: 'group',
        level: depth,
        docno: node.docno,
        parent: node.parent,
        acCode: '',
        description: node.description,
        totalDebit: 0,
        totalCredit: 0,
        netDebit: 0,
        netCredit: 0,
        hasChildren: true
      });
    }
    return hasLedger;
  }
  
  var roots = childrenMap[0] || [];
  for (var r0 = 0; r0 < roots.length; r0++) {
    var root = roots[r0];
    if (root.acType === 'GL' && ledgerInfo[root.acCode]) {
      pushLedgerRow(root, 1);
    } else {
      buildNode(root, 1);
    }
  }  
  // Safety net: include any GL ledger that could not be reached through the tree
  for (var ac3 in ledgerInfo) {
    if (ledgerInfo.hasOwnProperty(ac3) && !includedLedgers[ac3]) {
      var li = ledgerInfo[ac3];
      rows.push({
        type: 'ledger',
        level: 1,
        docno: li.docno,
        parent: 0,
        acCode: li.acCode,
        description: li.description,
        totalDebit: li.totalDebit,
        totalCredit: li.totalCredit,
        netDebit: li.netDebit,
        netCredit: li.netCredit,
        hasChildren: false
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Calculate summary over ledger rows only
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  var totalNetDebit = 0;
  var totalNetCredit = 0;
  for (var r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
    if (rows[r].type !== 'ledger') continue;
    totalDebit += rows[r].totalDebit;
    totalCredit += rows[r].totalCredit;
    totalNetDebit += rows[r].netDebit;
    totalNetCredit += rows[r].netCredit;
  }
  
  var result = {
    status: "success",
    rows: rows,
    summary: {
      totalAccounts: totalAccounts,
      totalDebit: Math.round(totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(totalCredit * 100) / 100,
      totalNetDebit: Math.round(totalNetDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalNetCredit: Math.round(totalNetCredit * 100) / 100,
      difference: Math.round((totalDebit - totalCredit) * 100) / 100,
      balanced: Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) < 0.01
    },
    fromDate: fromDate,
    toDate: toDate
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// PROFIT AND LOSS HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get Profit and Loss (income & expense GL accounts with totals for a date range)
function handleGetProfitAndLossGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read the full COA hierarchy (groups + GL ledgers)
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  var coaByDocno = {}; // docno -> account record
  var coaMap = {};     // acCode -> account record
  if (coaSheet) {
    var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
      var acCode = String(coaData[i][0] || '');
      var acType = String(coaData[i][1] || '');
      var desc = coaData[i][2] || '';
      var level = parseInt(coaData[i][3]) || 1;
      var parent = parseInt(coaData[i][4]) || 0;
      var docno = parseInt(coaData[i][5]) || 0;
      var firstLevel = parseInt(coaData[i][6]) || 0;
      if (!acCode && !docno) continue;
      
      var rec = {
        acCode: acCode, acType: acType, description: desc,
        level: level, parent: parent, docno: docno, firstLevel: firstLevel
      };
      if (docno) coaByDocno[docno] = rec;
      if (acCode) coaMap[acCode] = rec;
    }
  }
  
  // Resolve the FIRST_LEVEL classification of an account by walking up the
  // parent chain to the top-level account that carries it.
  // 1=ASSET, 2=LIABILITY, 3=EQUITY, 4=INCOME, 5=EXPENSE
  function getAccountFirstLevel(rec) {
    var current = rec;
    var steps = 0;
    while (current && steps < 50) {
      steps++;
      if (current.firstLevel) return current.firstLevel;
      if (!current.parent || current.parent == 0) {
        return current.firstLevel || 0;
      }
      current = coaByDocno[current.parent];
    }
    return (current && current.firstLevel) ? current.firstLevel : (rec.firstLevel || 0);
  }
  
  // Get AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  var totalsMap = {}; // acCode -> { totalDebit, totalCredit }
  
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var acDocNo = String(data[i][3] || '');
      if (!acDocNo) continue;
      
      // Apply date range filter on VALDATE (index 6)
      var valDate = '';
      if (data[i][6]) {
        try {
          valDate = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][6]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
        } catch (e) {
          valDate = '';
        }
      }
      if (fromDate && valDate < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && valDate > toDate) continue;
      
      var amount = parseFloat(data[i][8]) || 0;
      
      // Only include accounts that exist in COA as GL accounts
      var coaInfo = coaMap[acDocNo];
      if (!coaInfo || coaInfo.acType !== 'GL') continue;
      
      if (!totalsMap[acDocNo]) {
        totalsMap[acDocNo] = { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
      }
      
      if (amount < 0) {
        totalsMap[acDocNo].totalDebit += Math.abs(amount);
      } else {
        totalsMap[acDocNo].totalCredit += amount;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // P&L ledgers: all GL accounts whose FIRST_LEVEL classification is
  // INCOME (4) or EXPENSE (5).
  var pnlCodes = {}; // acCode -> { section, firstLevel }
  for (var code in coaMap) {
    if (!coaMap.hasOwnProperty(code)) continue;
    var rec = coaMap[code];
    if (rec.acType !== 'GL') continue;
    var fl = getAccountFirstLevel(rec);
    if (fl === 4 || fl === 5) {
      pnlCodes[code] = { section: fl === 4 ? 'income' : 'expense', firstLevel: fl };
    }
  }
  
  // Compute per-ledger figures
  var ledgerInfo = {}; // acCode -> ledger figures + hierarchy info
  var incomeAccounts = 0;
  var expenseAccounts = 0;
  for (var ac in pnlCodes) {
    if (!pnlCodes.hasOwnProperty(ac)) continue;
    var info = coaMap[ac];
    if (!info) continue;
    var t = totalsMap[ac] || { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
    var netDebit = 0;
    var netCredit = 0;
    var net = t.totalDebit - t.totalCredit;
    if (net > 0) {
      netDebit = net;
    } else {
      netCredit = Math.abs(net);
    }
    // P&L amount: income = credit - debit, expense = debit - credit
    var section = pnlCodes[ac].section;
    var pnlAmount = section === 'income' ? (t.totalCredit - t.totalDebit) : (t.totalDebit - t.totalCredit);
    ledgerInfo[ac] = {
      acCode: ac,
      description: info.description,
      level: info.level,
      parent: info.parent,
      docno: info.docno,
      section: section,
      firstLevel: pnlCodes[ac].firstLevel,
      totalDebit: Math.round(t.totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(t.totalCredit * 100) / 100,
      netDebit: Math.round(netDebit * 100) / 100,
      netCredit: Math.round(netCredit * 100) / 100,
      pnlAmount: Math.round(pnlAmount * 100) / 100
    };
    if (section === 'income') {
      incomeAccounts++;
    } else {
      expenseAccounts++;
    }
  }
  
  // Build parent -> children map from the full COA hierarchy
  var childrenMap = {};
  for (var d in coaByDocno) {
    if (coaByDocno.hasOwnProperty(d)) {
      var r = coaByDocno[d];
      var p = r.parent || 0;
      if (!childrenMap[p]) childrenMap[p] = [];
      childrenMap[p].push(r);
    }
  }
  for (var cm in childrenMap) {
    if (childrenMap.hasOwnProperty(cm)) {
      childrenMap[cm].sort(function(a, b) { return a.docno - b.docno; });
    }
  }
  
  // Walk the COA tree and emit P&L rows in tree order.
  // Group rows are headers only (no amounts); only groups that lead to at
  // least one P&L ledger are included.
  var rows = [];
  var includedLedgers = {}; // acCode -> true
  
  function pushLedgerRow(rec, depth) {
    var lr = ledgerInfo[rec.acCode];
    if (!lr) return false;
    rows.push({
      type: 'ledger',
      level: depth,
      docno: rec.docno,
      parent: rec.parent,
      acCode: rec.acCode,
      description: rec.description,
      section: lr.section,
      totalDebit: lr.totalDebit,
      totalCredit: lr.totalCredit,
      netDebit: lr.netDebit,
      netCredit: lr.netCredit,
      pnlAmount: lr.pnlAmount,
      hasChildren: false
    });
    includedLedgers[rec.acCode] = true;
    return true;
  }
  
  function buildNode(node, depth) {
    var kids = childrenMap[node.docno] || [];
    var startIdx = rows.length;
    var hasLedger = false;
    for (var k = 0; k < kids.length; k++) {
      var child = kids[k];
      if (child.acType === 'GL' && ledgerInfo[child.acCode]) {
        if (pushLedgerRow(child, depth + 1)) hasLedger = true;
      } else {
        if (buildNode(child, depth + 1)) hasLedger = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasLedger && node.acType !== 'GL') {
      rows.splice(startIdx, 0, {
        type: 'group',
        level: depth,
        docno: node.docno,
        parent: node.parent,
        acCode: '',
        description: node.description,
        section: node.firstLevel === 4 ? 'income' : (node.firstLevel === 5 ? 'expense' : ''),
        totalDebit: 0,
        totalCredit: 0,
        netDebit: 0,
        netCredit: 0,
        pnlAmount: 0,
        hasChildren: true
      });
    }
    return hasLedger;
  }
  
  var roots = childrenMap[0] || [];
  for (var r0 = 0; r0 < roots.length; r0++) {
    var root = roots[r0];
    if (root.acType === 'GL' && ledgerInfo[root.acCode]) {
      pushLedgerRow(root, 1);
    } else {
      buildNode(root, 1);
    }
  }
  // Safety net: include any P&L ledger that could not be reached through the tree
  for (var ac3 in ledgerInfo) {
    if (ledgerInfo.hasOwnProperty(ac3) && !includedLedgers[ac3]) {
      var li = ledgerInfo[ac3];
      rows.push({
        type: 'ledger',
        level: 1,
        docno: li.docno,
        parent: 0,
        acCode: li.acCode,
        description: li.description,
        section: li.section,
        totalDebit: li.totalDebit,
        totalCredit: li.totalCredit,
        netDebit: li.netDebit,
        netCredit: li.netCredit,
        pnlAmount: li.pnlAmount,
        hasChildren: false
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Calculate summary over ledger rows only
  var totalIncome = 0;
  var totalExpenses = 0;
  for (var r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
    if (rows[r].type !== 'ledger') continue;
    if (rows[r].section === 'income') {
      totalIncome += rows[r].pnlAmount;
    } else {
      totalExpenses += rows[r].pnlAmount;
    }
  }
  var netProfit = Math.round((totalIncome - totalExpenses) * 100) / 100;
  
  var result = {
    status: "success",
    rows: rows,
    summary: {
      incomeAccounts: incomeAccounts,
      expenseAccounts: expenseAccounts,
      totalIncome: Math.round(totalIncome * 100) / 100,
      totalExpenses: Math.round(totalExpenses * 100) / 100,
      netProfit: netProfit,
      isProfit: netProfit >= 0
    },
    fromDate: fromDate,
    toDate: toDate
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// BALANCE SHEET HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get balance sheet (asset, liability and equity GL accounts with totals for a date range)
function handleGetBalanceSheetGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read the full COA hierarchy (groups + GL ledgers)
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  var coaByDocno = {}; // docno -> account record
  var coaMap = {};     // acCode -> account record
  if (coaSheet) {
    var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
      var acCode = String(coaData[i][0] || '');
      var acType = String(coaData[i][1] || '');
      var desc = coaData[i][2] || '';
      var level = parseInt(coaData[i][3]) || 1;
      var parent = parseInt(coaData[i][4]) || 0;
      var docno = parseInt(coaData[i][5]) || 0;
      var firstLevel = parseInt(coaData[i][6]) || 0;
      if (!acCode && !docno) continue;
      
      var rec = {
        acCode: acCode, acType: acType, description: desc,
        level: level, parent: parent, docno: docno, firstLevel: firstLevel
      };
      if (docno) coaByDocno[docno] = rec;
      if (acCode) coaMap[acCode] = rec;
    }
  }
  
  // Resolve the FIRST_LEVEL classification of an account by walking up the
  // parent chain to the top-level account that carries it.
  // 1=ASSET, 2=LIABILITY, 3=EQUITY, 4=INCOME, 5=EXPENSE
  function getAccountFirstLevel(rec) {
    var current = rec;
    var steps = 0;
    while (current && steps < 50) {
      steps++;
      if (current.firstLevel) return current.firstLevel;
      if (!current.parent || current.parent == 0) {
        return current.firstLevel || 0;
      }
      current = coaByDocno[current.parent];
    }
    return (current && current.firstLevel) ? current.firstLevel : (rec.firstLevel || 0);
  }
  
  // Get AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  var totalsMap = {}; // acCode -> { totalDebit, totalCredit }
  
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var acDocNo = String(data[i][3] || '');
      if (!acDocNo) continue;
      
      // Apply date range filter on VALDATE (index 6)
      var valDate = '';
      if (data[i][6]) {
        try {
          valDate = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][6]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
        } catch (e) {
          valDate = '';
        }
      }
      if (fromDate && valDate < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && valDate > toDate) continue;
      
      var amount = parseFloat(data[i][8]) || 0;
      
      // Only include accounts that exist in COA as GL accounts
      var coaInfo = coaMap[acDocNo];
      if (!coaInfo || coaInfo.acType !== 'GL') continue;
      
      if (!totalsMap[acDocNo]) {
        totalsMap[acDocNo] = { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
      }
      
      if (amount < 0) {
        totalsMap[acDocNo].totalDebit += Math.abs(amount);
      } else {
        totalsMap[acDocNo].totalCredit += amount;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Balance sheet ledgers: all GL accounts whose FIRST_LEVEL classification is
  // ASSET (1), LIABILITY (2) or EQUITY (3).
  var bsCodes = {}; // acCode -> { section, firstLevel }
  for (var code in coaMap) {
    if (!coaMap.hasOwnProperty(code)) continue;
    var rec = coaMap[code];
    if (rec.acType !== 'GL') continue;
    var fl = getAccountFirstLevel(rec);
    if (fl === 1 || fl === 2 || fl === 3) {
      var section = fl === 1 ? 'asset' : (fl === 2 ? 'liability' : 'equity');
      bsCodes[code] = { section: section, firstLevel: fl };
    }
  }
  
  // Net Profit/(Loss) for the period from P&L accounts (INCOME 4 / EXPENSE 5)
  var totalIncome = 0;
  var totalExpenses = 0;
  for (var codePl in coaMap) {
    if (!coaMap.hasOwnProperty(codePl)) continue;
    var recPl = coaMap[codePl];
    if (recPl.acType !== 'GL') continue;
    var flPl = getAccountFirstLevel(recPl);
    if (flPl !== 4 && flPl !== 5) continue;
    var tPl = totalsMap[codePl] || { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
    if (flPl === 4) {
      totalIncome += (tPl.totalCredit - tPl.totalDebit);
    } else {
      totalExpenses += (tPl.totalDebit - tPl.totalCredit);
    }
  }
  var netProfit = Math.round((totalIncome - totalExpenses) * 100) / 100;
  
  // Compute per-ledger figures
  var ledgerInfo = {}; // acCode -> ledger figures + hierarchy info
  var assetAccounts = 0;
  var liabilityAccounts = 0;
  var equityAccounts = 0;
  for (var ac in bsCodes) {
    if (!bsCodes.hasOwnProperty(ac)) continue;
    var info = coaMap[ac];
    if (!info) continue;
    var t = totalsMap[ac] || { totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 };
    var netDebit = 0;
    var netCredit = 0;
    var net = t.totalDebit - t.totalCredit;
    if (net > 0) {
      netDebit = net;
    } else {
      netCredit = Math.abs(net);
    }
    var section = bsCodes[ac].section;
    ledgerInfo[ac] = {
      acCode: ac,
      description: info.description,
      level: info.level,
      parent: info.parent,
      docno: info.docno,
      section: section,
      firstLevel: bsCodes[ac].firstLevel,
      totalDebit: Math.round(t.totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(t.totalCredit * 100) / 100,
      netDebit: Math.round(netDebit * 100) / 100,
      netCredit: Math.round(netCredit * 100) / 100
    };
    if (section === 'asset') {
      assetAccounts++;
    } else if (section === 'liability') {
      liabilityAccounts++;
    } else {
      equityAccounts++;
    }
  }
  
  // Build parent -> children map from the full COA hierarchy
  var childrenMap = {};
  for (var d in coaByDocno) {
    if (coaByDocno.hasOwnProperty(d)) {
      var r = coaByDocno[d];
      var p = r.parent || 0;
      if (!childrenMap[p]) childrenMap[p] = [];
      childrenMap[p].push(r);
    }
  }
  for (var cm in childrenMap) {
    if (childrenMap.hasOwnProperty(cm)) {
      childrenMap[cm].sort(function(a, b) { return a.docno - b.docno; });
    }
  }
  
  // Walk the COA tree and emit balance sheet rows in tree order.
  // Group rows are headers only (no amounts); only groups that lead to at
  // least one balance sheet ledger are included.
  var rows = [];
  var includedLedgers = {}; // acCode -> true
  
  function pushLedgerRow(rec, depth) {
    var lr = ledgerInfo[rec.acCode];
    if (!lr) return false;
    rows.push({
      type: 'ledger',
      level: depth,
      docno: rec.docno,
      parent: rec.parent,
      acCode: rec.acCode,
      description: rec.description,
      section: lr.section,
      totalDebit: lr.totalDebit,
      totalCredit: lr.totalCredit,
      netDebit: lr.netDebit,
      netCredit: lr.netCredit,
      hasChildren: false
    });
    includedLedgers[rec.acCode] = true;
    return true;
  }
  
  function buildNode(node, depth) {
    var kids = childrenMap[node.docno] || [];
    var startIdx = rows.length;
    var hasLedger = false;
    for (var k = 0; k < kids.length; k++) {
      var child = kids[k];
      if (child.acType === 'GL' && ledgerInfo[child.acCode]) {
        if (pushLedgerRow(child, depth + 1)) hasLedger = true;
      } else {
        if (buildNode(child, depth + 1)) hasLedger = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasLedger && node.acType !== 'GL') {
      rows.splice(startIdx, 0, {
        type: 'group',
        level: depth,
        docno: node.docno,
        parent: node.parent,
        acCode: '',
        description: node.description,
        section: node.firstLevel === 1 ? 'asset' : (node.firstLevel === 2 ? 'liability' : (node.firstLevel === 3 ? 'equity' : '')),
        totalDebit: 0,
        totalCredit: 0,
        netDebit: 0,
        netCredit: 0,
        hasChildren: true
      });
    }
    return hasLedger;
  }
  
  var roots = childrenMap[0] || [];
  for (var r0 = 0; r0 < roots.length; r0++) {
    var root = roots[r0];
    if (root.acType === 'GL' && ledgerInfo[root.acCode]) {
      pushLedgerRow(root, 1);
    } else {
      buildNode(root, 1);
    }
  }
  // Safety net: include any balance sheet ledger that could not be reached through the tree
  for (var ac3 in ledgerInfo) {
    if (ledgerInfo.hasOwnProperty(ac3) && !includedLedgers[ac3]) {
      var li = ledgerInfo[ac3];
      rows.push({
        type: 'ledger',
        level: 1,
        docno: li.docno,
        parent: 0,
        acCode: li.acCode,
        description: li.description,
        section: li.section,
        totalDebit: li.totalDebit,
        totalCredit: li.totalCredit,
        netDebit: li.netDebit,
        netCredit: li.netCredit,
        hasChildren: false
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Calculate summary over ledger rows only
  var totalAssets = 0;
  var totalLiabilities = 0;
  var totalEquity = 0;
  for (var r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
    if (rows[r].type !== 'ledger') continue;
    if (rows[r].section === 'asset') {
      totalAssets += (rows[r].netDebit - rows[r].netCredit);
    } else if (rows[r].section === 'liability') {
      totalLiabilities += (rows[r].netCredit - rows[r].netDebit);
    } else {
      totalEquity += (rows[r].netCredit - rows[r].netDebit);
    }
  }
  var totalLiabilitiesEquity = Math.round((totalLiabilities + totalEquity + netProfit) * 100) / 100;
  var difference = Math.round((totalAssets - totalLiabilitiesEquity) * 100) / 100;
  
  var result = {
    status: "success",
    rows: rows,
    summary: {
      assetAccounts: assetAccounts,
      liabilityAccounts: liabilityAccounts,
      equityAccounts: equityAccounts,
      totalAssets: Math.round(totalAssets * 100) / 100,
      totalLiabilities: Math.round(totalLiabilities * 100) / 100,
      totalEquity: Math.round(totalEquity * 100) / 100,
      netProfit: netProfit,
      totalLiabilitiesEquity: totalLiabilitiesEquity,
      difference: difference,
      balanced: Math.abs(difference) < 0.01
    },
    fromDate: fromDate,
    toDate: toDate
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// CUSTOMER SALES SUMMARY HANDLER (DASHBOARD)
// ============================================


// Get sales summary grouped by customer for the current month
function handleGetCustomerSalesSummaryGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Calculate current month date range
  var now = new Date();
  var year = now.getFullYear();
  var month = now.getMonth(); // 0-based
  var firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
  var lastDay = new Date(year, month + 1, 0); // Last day of current month
  
  var fromDate = Utilities.formatDate(firstDay, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  var toDate = Utilities.formatDate(lastDay, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  
  // Build customer lookup map (code -> description)
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var customerMap = {};
  for (var c = 1; c < customerData.length; c++) {
    if (customerData[c][0]) {
      customerMap[String(customerData[c][0]).toLowerCase()] = customerData[c][1] || '';
    }
  }
  
  // Get all sales from SalesHeader sheet
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Aggregate sales by customer
  var salesByCustomer = {};
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var dateObj = salesHeaderData[i][2] ? new Date(salesHeaderData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Filter by current month
      if (dateStr < fromDate || dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      var customerCode = String(salesHeaderData[i][3] || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!customerCode) continue;
      
      var grossAmount = parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][5]) || 0;
      var netAmount = parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][9]) || 0;
      
      if (!salesByCustomer[customerCode]) {
        salesByCustomer[customerCode] = {
          customerCode: customerCode,
          customerName: customerMap[customerCode] || customerCode,
          totalGross: 0,
          totalNet: 0,
          invoiceCount: 0
        };
      }
      
      salesByCustomer[customerCode].totalGross += grossAmount;
      salesByCustomer[customerCode].totalNet += netAmount;
      salesByCustomer[customerCode].invoiceCount++;
    }
  }
  
  // Convert to array and sort by totalGross descending
  var summary = [];
  for (var key in salesByCustomer) {
    if (salesByCustomer.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
      var item = salesByCustomer[key];
      item.totalGross = Math.round(item.totalGross * 100) / 100;
      item.totalNet = Math.round(item.totalNet * 100) / 100;
      summary.push(item);
    }
  }
  
  summary.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.totalGross - a.totalGross;
  });
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    summary: summary,
    month: month + 1,
    year: year,
    fromDate: fromDate,
    toDate: toDate
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// ============================================
